  • 总共包括 1,614 人:其中 960 人来自里约热内卢,654 人来自里斯本。疫情曲线显示出类似的上升和下降模式,里斯本的峰值较早(2022年6月),里约热内卢的峰值较晚(2022年8月)。皮肤或粘膜病变是这两种情况下最常报告的表现,影响超过 85% 的个体。里约热内卢更常报告发烧,而里斯本更常报告淋巴结病和乏力。
  • 猴痘病毒,特别是进化枝 Ib 和进化枝 IIb,具有不同的 传播 模式和疾病严重程度概况。进化枝 Ib 似乎传播得更广泛,影响非性人群,而进化枝 IIb 主要通过性传播。该病毒还采用免疫逃避策略,例如破坏干扰素信号传导和创建诱饵受体。

  • Median age was 30 years, and 73/155 (47%) had HIV infection. Multisite pain, particularly anogenital, was highly prevalent. [10.1016/j.ebiom.2026.106409].
  • The supplied evidence does not clearly state a directly observed main result. [10.1002/advs.76926].
  • A total of 1,614 individuals were included: 960 from Rio de Janeiro and 654 from Lisbon.

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期猴痘病毒(Mpox病毒)新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 世卫组织非洲区域办事处报告乌干达确诊一例 猴痘 暴发,过去 24 小时报告新增 37 例病例。累计确诊324例,死亡1例。 [news-f466817f7963c6c7]。
  • UKHSA 报告确认,截至 2026 年 7 月 31 日,英国已有 119 例 猴痘 Ib 分支病例,其中大多数病例发生在伦敦。它还注意到欧洲和北美通过 GBMSM 网络发现的社区 传播 以及 2025 年 12 月在英国发现的重组 猴痘病毒。
  • Bavarian Nordic 已获得欧盟批准,将其 猴痘/天花 疫苗 的使用范围扩大到 2 岁儿童。 [news-4706289f514a7fa4]。
  • 欧盟委员会授权 Bavarian Nordic 的 IMVANEX 猴痘 和天花 疫苗 用于 2 岁至 12 岁以下儿童,这标志着非复制 猴痘 疫苗 首次正式获准用于该年龄段。该决定基于在刚果民主共和国和乌干达进行的 2 期临床研究 (NCT06549530)。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Mpox virus news developments.
  • The WHO Regional Office for Africa reported a confirmed mpox outbreak in Uganda, with thirty seven new cases reported in the past 24 hours. Cumulatively, 324 confirmed cases and one death have been registered.
  • The UKHSA report confirmed 119 mpox clade Ib cases in the UK up to 31 July 2026, with most cases in London.
  • Bavarian Nordic has received EU approval to expand the use of its mpox/smallpox vaccine to children as young as 2 years old. [news-4706289f514a7fa4].
  • The European Commission authorized Bavarian Nordic's IMVANEX mpox and smallpox vaccine for children aged 2 to under 12 years, marking the first time a non-replicating mpox vaccine was formally cleared for this age group.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: eBioMedicine  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-05  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.ebiom.2026.106409  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 131, 106409
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

住院分支 Ib 猴痘 中的病变病毒负荷、多重耐药性重复感染和 HIV 相关血液学脆弱性:乌干达的一项前瞻性 队列研究 研究

Lesion viral burden, multidrug-resistant superinfection, and HIV-associated haematological vulnerability in hospitalised clade Ib mpox: a prospective cohort study in Uganda
作者： Jennifer Serwanga, Raymond Ernest Kaweesa, Geoffrey Odoch, Brenda Nairuba, Deborah Mukisa, Rodney Abraham Tumusiime, Ruth Priscilla Nambi, Annie Daphine Ntabadde, Violet Ankunda, Tom Lutalo
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘 已在非洲转向持续的人对人 传播,但纳入病毒负荷、细菌合并感染、抗菌素耐药性 (AMR)、HIV 状况和常规生物标志物的综合分类仍然很少。
设计与对象
在乌干达国家 猴痘 转诊医院,我们前瞻性地招募了症状出现后 14 ± 2 天的 155 名成年人;猴痘 通过病灶拭子 qPCR (F3L) 得到证实,并通过预先指定子集中的全 基因组 测序进行进化枝分配(2025 年 3 月至 4 月)。
核心方法
使用 qPCR 循环阈值 (Ct) 值估计病变病毒 DNA 负荷。对化脓性病变进行 EUCAST 标准化培养和药敏试验。常规实验室评估包括全血细胞计数、C 反应蛋白、血清化学、HIV 血清状态和血浆 HIV-1 RNA。
主要结果
Median age was 30 years, and 73/155 (47%) had HIV infection. Multisite pain, particularly anogenital, was highly prevalent. Among 80 participants with purulent lesions selected for clinically suspected bacterial superinfection, all yielded bacterial growth; 35/80 (43.8%) were polymicrobial and predominantly multidrug-resistant Gram-negative bacilli. Susceptibility to first-line β-lactams and fluoroquinolones was low, whereas meropenem retained activity (51/61, 84%). Lesion viral burden did not differ by HIV serostatus and showed weak correlation with HIV-1 viraemia; higher burden was associated with leucocytosis, neutrophilia with left shift, elevated CRP, and hypoalbuminaemia.
解释与创新
乌干达住院的急性 Ib 猴痘 成人患者表现出高病变病毒负荷、频繁的多重耐药细菌共同分离以及 HIV-1 感染者的炎症血液学特征。这些发现支持考虑将诊断微生物学、HIV 病毒载量评估和抗菌药物管理纳入流行病环境中的 猴痘 病例管理中。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,在 猴痘 管理中需要采用综合诊断方法,特别是在 HIV 患病率 较高且多重耐药细菌合并感染常见的环境中。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到单中心设计和潜在选择偏倚的限制,但由于前瞻性的 队列研究 方法和详细的微生物学和病毒学评估,证据充足。

Lesion viral burden, multidrug-resistant superinfection, and HIV-associated haematological vulnerability in hospitalised clade Ib mpox: a prospective cohort study in Uganda

Authors: Jennifer Serwanga, Raymond Ernest Kaweesa, Geoffrey Odoch, Brenda Nairuba, Deborah Mukisa, Rodney Abraham Tumusiime, Ruth Priscilla Nambi, Annie Daphine Ntabadde, Violet Ankunda, Tom Lutalo
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Mpox has shifted to sustained human-to-human transmission across Africa, yet integrated triage incorporating viral burden, bacterial co-infection, antimicrobial resistance (AMR), HIV status, and routine biomarkers remain scarce. METHODS: At Uganda's national mpox referral hospital, we prospectively enrolled 155 adults at 14 ± 2 days post-symptom onset; mpox was confirmed by lesion-swab qPCR (F3L), with clade assignment by whole-genome sequencing in a prespecified subset (March-April 2025). Lesion viral DNA burden was estimated using qPCR cycle threshold (Ct) values. Purulent lesions underwent EUCAST-standardised culture and susceptibility testing. Routine laboratory assessments included complete blood counts, C-reactive protein, serum chemistries, HIV serostatus, and plasma HIV-1 RNA. FINDINGS: Median age was 30 years, and 73/155 (47%) had HIV infection. Multisite pain, particularly anogenital, was highly prevalent. Among 80 participants with purulent lesions selected for clinically suspected bacterial superinfection, all yielded bacterial growth; 35/80 (43.8%) were polymicrobial and predominantly multidrug-resistant Gram-negative bacilli. Susceptibility to first-line β-lactams and fluoroquinolones was low, whereas meropenem retained activity (51/61, 84%). Lesion viral burden did not differ by HIV serostatus and showed weak correlation with HIV-1 viraemia; higher burden was associated with leucocytosis, neutrophilia with left shift, elevated CRP, and hypoalbuminaemia. Genomes clustered within Clade Ib, without segregation by HIV status or clinical severity. INTERPRETATION: Hospitalised adults with acute Clade Ib mpox in Uganda exhibited high lesion viral burden, frequent multidrug-resistant bacterial co-isolation, and an inflammatory haematological profile accentuated in participants living with HIV-1. These findings support consideration of integrating diagnostic microbiology, HIV viral load assessment, and antimicrobial stewardship into mpox case-management in endemic settings. FUNDING: This study was supported by the Coalition for Epidemic Preparedness Innovations (CEPI; Project ID PRJ-8284).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Mpox has shifted to sustained human-to-human transmission across Africa, yet integrated triage incorporating viral burden, bacterial co-infection, antimicrobial resistance (AMR), HIV status, and routine biomarkers remain scarce.
Design and population
At Uganda's national mpox referral hospital, we prospectively enrolled 155 adults at 14 ± 2 days post-symptom onset; mpox was confirmed by lesion-swab qPCR (F3L), with clade assignment by whole-genome sequencing in a prespecified subset (March-April 2025).
Core methods
Lesion viral DNA burden was estimated using qPCR cycle threshold (Ct) values. Purulent lesions underwent EUCAST-standardised culture and susceptibility testing. Routine laboratory assessments included complete blood counts, C-reactive protein, serum chemistries, HIV serostatus, and plasma HIV-1 RNA.
Main results
Median age was 30 years, and 73/155 (47%) had HIV infection. Multisite pain, particularly anogenital, was highly prevalent. Among 80 participants with purulent lesions selected for clinically suspected bacterial superinfection, all yielded bacterial growth; 35/80 (43.8%) were polymicrobial and predominantly multidrug-resistant Gram-negative bacilli. Susceptibility to first-line β-lactams and fluoroquinolones was low, whereas meropenem retained activity (51/61, 84%). Lesion viral burden did not differ by HIV serostatus and showed weak correlation with HIV-1 viraemia; higher burden was associated with leucocytosis, neutrophilia with left shift, elevated CRP, and hypoalbuminaemia.
Interpretation and novelty
Hospitalised adults with acute Clade Ib mpox in Uganda exhibited high lesion viral burden, frequent multidrug-resistant bacterial co-isolation, and an inflammatory haematological profile accentuated in participants living with HIV-1. These findings support consideration of integrating diagnostic microbiology, HIV viral load assessment, and antimicrobial stewardship into mpox case-management in endemic settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for integrated diagnostic approaches in mpox management, particularly in settings where HIV prevalence is high and multidrug-resistant bacterial co-infections are common.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its single-centre design and potential for selection bias, but the evidence is strong due to the prospective cohort approach and detailed microbiological and virological assessments.

中国大陆首例境外输入猴痘案件后新浪微博上的实时情绪动态和公众话语:情绪分析研究

Real-time emotional dynamics and public discourse on Sina Weibo following the first imported mpox case in mainland China: A sentiment analysis study
作者： Zhuoxiao Li, Yi Li, Ke Li, Chunbei Zhou, Hong Xiao
摘要中文翻译背景:人类 猴痘 已成为重大的公共卫生威胁;然而,对于中国公众对 猴痘 的实时情绪反应知之甚少。方法:收集中国大陆首例输入性猴痘病例公布后48小时内(2022年9月16日)新浪微博上包含关键词“重庆猴痘”的前40条热门帖子的评论。使用自然语言处理 (NLP),我​​们应用 BosonNLP 情感词典为每条评论分配情感值 (EV)。使用潜在狄利克雷分配模型来识别关键的公众关注点,并进行 Spearman 相关分析来检查参与度指标(评论、点赞、回复)和 EV 之间的关系。结果:40 个热门帖子分别收到 5,107 条评论、144,940 条点赞和 7,358 条回复。微博用户评论在公告发布后两小时内达到峰值(每小时数据),四小时后明显下降。评论最多的用户来自广东、上海、北京、浙江和重庆。公众最关心的是猴痘的态度和猴痘模式。情绪分析显示,评论大多为负面(76.13%)。负面评论的EV是正面评论的32.31倍。评论、点赞和回复的数量均与 EV 显着相关 (p < 0.001)。结论:中国大陆首例输入性 猴痘 病例后,公众情绪主要为负面,且高度集中在前四个小时内。调查结果强调,在新兴的 暴发 期间,需要进行及时、区域敏感的风险沟通。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解公众对中国大陆首例输入性 猴痘 病例的情绪反应,因为对该地区公众对 猴痘 的实时情绪知之甚少。
设计与对象
该研究收集了2022年9月16日中国大陆首例输入性猴痘病例公布后48小时内新浪微博上包含关键词“重庆猴痘”的前40条热门帖子的评论。样本包括来自广东、上海、北京、浙江和重庆的用户。
核心方法
使用自然语言处理(NLP)来分析评论,应用BosonNLP情感词典来分配情感值。潜在狄利克雷分配模型确定了关键的公众关注点,斯皮尔曼相关分析检查了参与度指标和情感价值之间的关系。
主要结果
这 40 条热门帖子收到了 5,107 条评论、144,940 条点赞和 7,358 条回复。评论在公告发布后两小时内达到顶峰,并在四小时后下降。公众最关心的是猴痘的态度和猴痘的模式。情绪分析显示,负面评论居多(76.13%),负面EV是正面评论的32.31倍。评论、点赞和回复的数量均与 EV 显着相关 (p < 0.001)。
解释与创新
调查结果突显了中国大陆首例输入性“猴痘”病例后公众的强烈而短暂的担忧,其重点明显集中在态度和“猴痘”模式上。这项研究为新兴 猴痘 暴发 期间公众情绪的动态提供了新颖的见解。
科研与公卫意义
该研究强调在 猴痘 暴发 期间进行及时、区域敏感的风险沟通的必要性,为公共卫生策略提供信息。它还强调了监测公众言论以更好地理解和管理公共卫生威胁的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对社交媒体数据的依赖而受到限制,而社交媒体数据可能无法代表更广泛的人群。由于首例进口 猴痘 案件的具体背景以及使用的情感词典可能无法完全捕捉微妙的公众情绪,证据的强度中等。

Real-time emotional dynamics and public discourse on Sina Weibo following the first imported mpox case in mainland China: A sentiment analysis study

Authors: Zhuoxiao Li, Yi Li, Ke Li, Chunbei Zhou, Hong Xiao
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: The human mpox has become a significant public health threat; however, little is known about real-time public emotional responses to mpox in China. METHODS: We collected comments from the top 40 popular posts containing the keyword "Chongqing mpox" on Sina Weibo within 48 hours following the announcement of the first imported mpox case in mainland China (September 16, 2022). Using natural language processing (NLP), we applied the BosonNLP sentiment dictionary to assign emotional values (EVs) to each comment. Latent Dirichlet Allocation model was used to identify key public concerns, and Spearman correlation analysis was conducted to examine relationships between engagement metrics (comments, likes, responses) and EVs. RESULTS: The 40 popular posts received 5,107 comments, 144,940 likes, and 7,358 responses, respectively. Weibo users' comments reached a peak within two hours after the announcement (hourly data) and decreased markedly after four hours. Users with the most comments came from Guangdong, Shanghai, Beijing, Zhejiang, and Chongqing. The public was most concerned about attitudes and the transmission mode of the mpox. Sentiment analysis showed that the comments were mostly negative (76.13%). The EVs of the negative comments were 32.31 times higher than those of the positive comments. The numbers of comments, likes, and responses were all significantly associated with EVs (p < 0.001). CONCLUSIONS: Public sentiment following the first imported mpox case in mainland China was predominantly negative and highly concentrated within the first four hours. Findings underscore the need for timely, region-sensitive risk communication during emerging outbreaks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the public emotional response to the first imported mpox case in mainland China, as little is known about real-time public sentiment towards mpox in this region.
Design and population
The study collected comments from the top 40 popular posts containing the keyword "Chongqing mpox" on Sina Weibo within 48 hours following the announcement of the first imported mpox case in mainland China on September 16, 2022. The sample included users from Guangdong, Shanghai, Beijing, Zhejiang, and Chongqing.
Core methods
Natural language processing (NLP) was used to analyze comments, applying the BosonNLP sentiment dictionary to assign emotional values. A Latent Dirichlet Allocation model identified key public concerns, and Spearman correlation analysis examined relationships between engagement metrics and emotional values.
Main results
The 40 popular posts received 5,107 comments, 144,940 likes, and 7,358 responses. Comments peaked within two hours after the announcement and declined after four hours. The public was most concerned about attitudes and transmission mode of mpox. Sentiment analysis showed that comments were mostly negative (76.13%), with negative EVs 32.31 times higher than positive ones. The numbers of comments, likes, and responses were all significantly associated with EVs (p < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the intense and short-lived public concern following the first imported mpox case in mainland China, with a clear focus on attitudes and transmission modes. This study provides novel insights into the dynamics of public sentiment during emerging mpox outbreaks.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study informs public health strategies by emphasizing the need for timely, region-sensitive risk communication during mpox outbreaks. It also underscores the importance of monitoring public discourse to better understand and manage public health threats.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on social media data, which may not represent the broader population. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the specific context of the first imported mpox case and the use of a sentiment dictionary that may not fully capture nuanced public emotions.

克服 传播 障碍:用于直接中和猴痘病毒和伤口愈合的双功能粘合水凝胶

Overcoming Transmission Barriers: A Dual‐Functional Adhesive Hydrogel for Direct Monkeypox Virus Neutralization and Wound Healing
作者： Ming Zhang, Jiaxin Tian, Xiaolong Zhu, Xiao Li, Xiangshu Qiu, Yuanyuan Li, Ben Zhong Tang, Ning Shi
摘要中文翻译摘要 猴痘病毒(MPXV)感染引起的局部炎症反应和持续的病毒复制给临床治疗带来了严峻的挑战。传统的治疗策略无法同时实现病毒清除和组织修复的双重目标。本研究创新性地采用“介孔载体锚定-凝胶基质嵌入”的分层组装策略构建功能化水凝胶(Polymer@mPDA@Gel)。采用介孔聚多巴胺(mPDA)作为专属锚定位点来负载聚合物,形成高效的光热组件,然后将该组件嵌入到具有强粘附力和良好生物相容性的凝胶基质中。通过分层组装,将光热性能、抗炎性能和生物界面特性精准整合,为其抗病毒、抗炎功能奠定核心基础。构建的功能化水凝胶实现了MPXV的靶向清除、炎症调节和组织修复一体化,为MPXV感染的临床治疗提供了新的材料技术路径和理论支持。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
提供的证据没有明确说明研究问题或其基本原理。
设计与对象
本研究创新性地采用“介孔载体锚定-凝胶基质嵌入”的分层组装策略构建功能化水凝胶(Polymer@mPDA@Gel)。
核心方法
采用介孔聚多巴胺(mPDA)作为专属锚定位点来负载聚合物,形成高效的光热组件,然后将该组件嵌入到具有强粘附力和良好生物相容性的凝胶基质中。
主要结果
提供的证据没有明确说明直接观察到的主要结果。
解释与创新
构建的功能化水凝胶实现了MPXV的靶向清除、炎症调节和组织修复一体化,为MPXV感染的临床治疗提供了新的材料技术路径和理论支持。
科研与公卫意义
摘要 猴痘病毒(MPXV)感染引起的局部炎症反应和持续的病毒复制给临床治疗带来了严峻的挑战。
局限与证据强度
通过分层组装,将光热性能、抗炎性能和生物界面特性精准整合,为其抗病毒、抗炎功能奠定核心基础。

Overcoming Transmission Barriers: A Dual‐Functional Adhesive Hydrogel for Direct Monkeypox Virus Neutralization and Wound Healing

Authors: Ming Zhang, Jiaxin Tian, Xiaolong Zhu, Xiao Li, Xiangshu Qiu, Yuanyuan Li, Ben Zhong Tang, Ning Shi
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT The local inflammatory response and ongoing viral replication caused by monkeypox virus (MPXV) infection pose severe challenges for clinical treatment. Traditional treatment strategies are unable to achieve the dual goals of virus clearance and tissue repair simultaneously. This study innovatively adopts a hierarchical assembly strategy of “mesoporous carrier anchoring‐gel matrix embedding” to construct a functionalized hydrogel (Polymer@mPDA@Gel). Using mesoporous polydopamine (mPDA) as the exclusive anchoring site to load the polymer, forming an efficient photothermal component, and then embedding this component in a gel matrix with strong adhesion and good biocompatibility. Through hierarchical assembly, the photothermal performance, anti‐inflammatory performance, and biointerfacial properties are precisely integrated to lay the core foundation for its anti‐viral and anti‐inflammatory functions. The functionalized hydrogel constructed realizes targeted clearance of MPXV, regulation of inflammation, and integration of tissue repair, providing a new material technology path and theoretical support for the clinical treatment of MPXV infection.

抗体与猴痘病毒 E8L 结合的计算拯救:从静电排斥到盐桥增强亲和力。

Computational rescue of antibody binding to monkeypox virus E8L: from electrostatic repulsion to salt bridge-enhanced affinity.
作者： Zhendong Li, Wenbin Qiu, Yanbiao Wang, John Z H Zhang
摘要中文翻译正在进行的 猴痘(猴痘)暴发 强调了对有效治疗抗体的需求。抗体 VACV-66 与痘苗病毒 D8 蛋白强烈结合,但与 MPXV 同源物 E8L 的交叉反应性显着降低,尽管序列同一性 >95%。在本研究中,我们结合AlphaFold3建模、分子动力学模拟和ASGB-IE自由能计算来研究这一机制,并合理设计针对E8L的高亲和力抗体突变。我们的计算表明,E8L 中的单个 N18D 突变(其中 D8 中的 N18 对应于 E8L 中的 D18)引入了与抗体残基 D34 L 的静电排斥,引发局部构象变化,将关键残基 R20 从 W106 H 和 Y33 L 形成的芳香笼中取代。基于这一机制,D34 L的饱和突变确定了三个最佳突变(D34 L R、D34 L K和D34 L N),它们通过消除静电斥力、恢复R20插入芳香笼并形成新的有利相互作用来增强结合自由能。对不同系统中残基 N18 和 R20 的结合自由能贡献的定量分析为该机制提供了额外的支持。这项工作阐明了单个病毒 突变 如何抑制抗体交叉反应性,并为针对新兴 正痘病毒 的机制引导抗体优化提供了通用计算策略。已鉴定的 D34 L R、D34 L K 和 D34 L N 突变体代表了实验验证和治疗开发的有希望的候选者。

正在进行的 猴痘(猴痘)暴发 强调了对有效治疗抗体的需求。
设计与对象
抗体 VACV-66 与痘苗病毒 D8 蛋白强烈结合,但与 MPXV 同源物 E8L 的交叉反应性显着降低,尽管序列同一性 >95%。
核心方法

主要结果
我们的计算表明,E8L 中的单个 N18D 突变(其中 D8 中的 N18 对应于 E8L 中的 D18)引入了与抗体残基 D34 L 的静电排斥,引发局部构象变化,将关键残基 R20 从 W106 H 和 Y33 L 形成的芳香笼中取代。

局限与证据强度
这项工作阐明了单个病毒 突变 如何抑制抗体交叉反应性,并为针对新兴 正痘病毒 的机制引导抗体优化提供了通用计算策略。

Computational rescue of antibody binding to monkeypox virus E8L: from electrostatic repulsion to salt bridge-enhanced affinity.

Authors: Zhendong Li, Wenbin Qiu, Yanbiao Wang, John Z H Zhang
Original abstract (English)
The ongoing Mpox (monkeypox) outbreak underscores the need for effective therapeutic antibodies. Antibody VACV-66 binds strongly to the vaccinia virus D8 protein but shows markedly reduced cross-reactivity to the MPXV homolog E8L, despite >95% sequence identity. In this study, we combined AlphaFold3 modeling, molecular dynamics simulations, and ASGB-IE free energy calculations to investigate this mechanism and rationally design high-affinity antibody mutations targeting E8L. Our calculations revealed that the single N18D mutation in E8L (where N18 in D8 corresponds to D18 in E8L) introduces electrostatic repulsion with antibody residue D34 L , triggering local conformational changes that displace key residue R20 from the aromatic cage formed by W106 H and Y33 L . Based on this mechanism, saturation mutation of D34 L identified three optimal mutations (D34 L R, D34 L K, and D34 L N) that enhance binding free energy by removing electrostatic repulsion, restoring R20 insertion into the aromatic cage, and forming new favorable interactions. Quantitative analysis of the binding free energy contributions of residues N18 and R20 across different systems provided additional support for this mechanism. This work elucidates how a single viral mutation dampens antibody cross-reactivity and provides a general computational strategy for mechanism-guided antibody optimization against emerging orthopoxviruses. The identified D34 L R, D34 L K, and D34 L N mutants represent promising candidates for experimental validation and therapeutic development.

Interpretation and novelty

深度对比:巴西里约热内卢和葡萄牙里斯本第一波猴痘

An in-depth comparison: first wave of mpox in Rio de Janeiro, Brazil and Lisbon, Portugal
作者： Raffaele Junior Aliberti, Mayara Secco Torres da Silva, Valdiléa Gonçalves Veloso, Beatriz Grinsztejn, Rosa Maria Figueiredo Teodósio, Mayumi Duarte Wakimoto
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 2022 年 猴痘 暴发 迅速蔓延到流行地区以外,影响了 传播 模式大致相似但公共卫生应对和 监测 系统不同的多个国家。我们比较了巴西里约热内卢和葡萄牙里斯本第一波疫情期间报告的实验室确诊的 猴痘 感染,特别关注人口特征、临床表现、猴痘 模式、住院情况和 HIV 状态的作用。方法 我们对巴西和葡萄牙国家通报系统定期收集的去识别化 监测 数据进行了回顾性分析。2022 年 5 月至 12 月期间报告的经实验室确诊为 猴痘 的成年人也被纳入其中。跨数据集的变量得到了协调,缺失的数据被保留而无需插补。按城市和艾滋病毒状况进行描述性分析。组比较酌情使用 Pearson 卡方检验、Fisher 精确检验、Mann-Whitney U 检验和两个比例 Z 检验。还探讨了数据完整性。结果 总共纳入 1,614 人:960 人来自里约热内卢,654 人来自里斯本。两个城市的平均年龄相似(34.5 岁 vs 34.9 岁),但里斯本的男性比例更高(99.4% vs 93.0%)。疫情曲线显示出类似的上升和下降模式,里斯本的峰值较早(2022年6月),里约热内卢的峰值较晚(2022年8月)。皮肤或粘膜病变是这两种情况下最常报告的表现,影响超过 85% 的个体。里约热内卢更常报告发烧,而里斯本更常报告淋巴结病和乏力。在每个城市内,艾滋病毒感染者和非艾滋病毒感染者的症状特征没有显着差异。两个城市的住院率总体较低(里约热内卢为 5.7%,里斯本为 5.4%),尽管里约热内卢的艾滋病毒感染者住院几率高于未感染艾滋病毒的人。里约热内卢的数据完整性总体上好于里斯本。结论 里约热内卢和里斯本经历了类似的第一波 猴痘 流行病,尽管时间、监测 完整性和卫生系统背景存在差异。HIV 状态似乎并未显着改变急性临床表现,而住院风险可能受到免疫状态和护理途径差异的影响。这些发现强调了协调一致的高质量 监测 对于支持更准确的跨国比较和更有针对性的公共卫生应对措施的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在比较巴西里约热内卢和葡萄牙里斯本的第一波 猴痘 暴发,以了解尽管流行病学趋势相似,但 传播 模式、公共卫生反应和临床特征的差异。
设计与对象
对巴西和葡萄牙国家通报系统定期收集的去识别化 猴痘 数据进行了回顾性分析,其中包括 2022 年 5 月至 12 月期间报告的经实验室确诊的 猴痘 感染的成年人。
核心方法
跨数据集的变量得到了协调,缺失的数据被保留而无需插补。按城市和艾滋病毒状况进行描述性分析,并酌情使用皮尔逊卡方检验、费舍尔精确检验、曼-惠特尼 U 检验和两个比例 Z 检验进行分组比较。
主要结果
总共包括 1,614 人:其中 960 人来自里约热内卢,654 人来自里斯本。疫情曲线显示出类似的上升和下降模式,里斯本的峰值较早(2022年6月),里约热内卢的峰值较晚(2022年8月)。皮肤或粘膜病变是这两种情况下最常报告的表现,影响超过 85% 的个体。里约热内卢更常报告发烧,而里斯本更常报告淋巴结病和乏力。
解释与创新
作者解释说,两个城市的第一波猴痘疫情具有相似的临床表现,但时间和监测完整性方面存在显着差异。他们还指出,艾滋病毒状况并没有显着改变急性临床表现,而住院风险可能受到免疫状况和护理途径的影响。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了协调一致的高质量 监测 对于支持更准确的跨国比较和更有针对性的公共卫生应对措施的重要性,特别是在了解免疫状态对住院风险的影响方面。
局限与证据强度
里约热内卢的数据完整性普遍优于里斯本,这可能会影响比较的可靠性。由于依赖常规收集的 监测 数据以及数据质量和代表性方面的潜在限制,证据是中等的。

An in-depth comparison: first wave of mpox in Rio de Janeiro, Brazil and Lisbon, Portugal

Authors: Raffaele Junior Aliberti, Mayara Secco Torres da Silva, Valdiléa Gonçalves Veloso, Beatriz Grinsztejn, Rosa Maria Figueiredo Teodósio, Mayumi Duarte Wakimoto
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background The 2022 mpox outbreak spread rapidly beyond endemic regions, affecting multiple countries with broadly similar transmission patterns but different public health responses and surveillance systems. We compared laboratory-confirmed mpox infections reported during the first epidemic wave in Rio de Janeiro, Brazil, and Lisbon, Portugal, with particular attention to demographic characteristics, clinical presentation, transmission patterns, hospitalization, and the role of HIV status. Methods We conducted a retrospective analysis of routinely collected, deidentified surveillance data from national notification systems in Brazil and Portugal. Adults with laboratory-confirmed mpox reported between May and December 2022 were included. Variables were harmonized across datasets, and missing data were retained without imputation. Descriptive analyses were performed by city and HIV status. Group comparisons used Pearson’s chi-square test, Fisher’s exact test, Mann-Whitney U test, and two proportion Z tests, as appropriate. Data completeness was also explored. Results A total of 1,614 individuals were included: 960 from Rio de Janeiro and 654 from Lisbon. Mean age was similar in both cities (34.5 vs 34.9 years), but Lisbon had a higher proportion of males (99.4% vs 93.0%). The epidemic curves showed a similar rise-and-decline pattern, with an earlier peak in Lisbon (June 2022) and a later peak in Rio de Janeiro (August 2022). Skin or mucosal lesions were the most frequently reported manifestation in both settings, affecting more than 85% of individuals. Fever was reported more often in Rio de Janeiro, whereas adenopathy and asthenia were more commonly recorded in Lisbon. Within each city, symptom profiles were not significantly different between people living with HIV and those not living with HIV. Hospitalization rates were low overall in both cities (5.7% in Rio de Janeiro and 5.4% in Lisbon), although people living with HIV in Rio de Janeiro had higher odds of hospitalization than those without HIV. Data completeness was generally better in Rio de Janeiro than in Lisbon. Conclusions Rio de Janeiro and Lisbon experienced similarly shaped first wave mpox epidemics, despite differences in timing, surveillance completeness, and health system context. HIV status did not appear to meaningfully modify acute clinical presentation, while hospitalization risk may have been influenced by differences in immune status and care pathways. These findings highlight the importance of harmonized, high-quality surveillance to support more accurate cross-country comparisons and better targeted public health responses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to compare the first wave of mpox outbreaks in Rio de Janeiro, Brazil, and Lisbon, Portugal, to understand differences in transmission patterns, public health responses, and clinical characteristics despite similar epidemiological trends.
Design and population
A retrospective analysis of routinely collected, deidentified surveillance data from national notification systems in Brazil and Portugal was conducted, including adults with laboratory-confirmed mpox infections reported between May and December 2022.
Core methods
Variables were harmonized across datasets, and missing data were retained without imputation. Descriptive analyses were performed by city and HIV status, with group comparisons using Pearson’s chi-square test, Fisher’s exact test, Mann-Whitney U test, and two proportion Z tests, as appropriate.
Main results
A total of 1,614 individuals were included: 960 from Rio de Janeiro and 654 from Lisbon. The epidemic curves showed a similar rise-and-decline pattern, with an earlier peak in Lisbon (June 2022) and a later peak in Rio de Janeiro (August 2022). Skin or mucosal lesions were the most frequently reported manifestation in both settings, affecting more than 85% of individuals. Fever was reported more often in Rio de Janeiro, whereas adenopathy and asthenia were more commonly recorded in Lisbon.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the first wave of mpox epidemics in both cities had similar clinical manifestations, but differences in timing and surveillance completeness were notable. They also noted that HIV status did not significantly modify acute clinical presentation, while hospitalization risk may have been influenced by immune status and care pathways.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings highlight the importance of harmonized, high-quality surveillance to support more accurate cross-country comparisons and better targeted public health responses, particularly in understanding the impact of immune status on hospitalization risk.
Limitations and evidence strength
Data completeness was generally better in Rio de Janeiro than in Lisbon, which may affect the reliability of comparisons. The evidence is moderate due to the reliance on routinely collected surveillance data and potential limitations in data quality and representativeness.

测量中等收入国家健康科学学生的猴痘知识、感知和健康威胁信念的仪器的心理测量特性

Psychometric properties of an instrument measuring monkeypox knowledge, perception, and beliefs of health threat in health science students in a middle-income country
作者： Oriana Rivera-Lozada, Miguel Ipanaqué-Zapata, Isabel Cristina Rivera-Lozada, Elvis Siprian Castro-Alzate, Hebe Hernández-Romero, Mariana Gómez-Rivera, César Antonio Bonilla-Asalde, Oriana Rivera‐Lozada, Mariana Gòmez-Rivera
摘要中文翻译目的:本研究旨在建立 2022 年秘鲁两所大学卫生领域学生健康威胁知识、感知和信念测量猴痘(猴痘)评估工具的因子结构和可靠性。方法使用的方法本质上是心理测量。关于 猴痘 知识的研究变量是使用 Ricco 等人开发的包含 17 项的调查问卷进行评估的,该调查问卷最初适用于卫生专业人员,后来适应了秘鲁的情况,并针对 416 名学生进行了调查。结果 结果显示了足够的拟合优度指标,RMSEA 和 SRMR 值低于 0.08,TLI 高于 0.90,证实了可接受的模型拟合。猴痘 知识的可靠性足够(KR20 = 0.70 和 α = 0.73),对健康威胁的感知显示出最高的可靠性(α = 0.88 和 Ω = 0.89)。结论 该仪器在测量秘鲁健康学生的 猴痘 知识、感知和健康威胁信念方面表现出足够的因素结构和可接受的可靠性,支持其在未来研究和公共卫生干预中的适用性。

Psychometric properties of an instrument measuring monkeypox knowledge, perception, and beliefs of health threat in health science students in a middle-income country

Authors: Oriana Rivera-Lozada, Miguel Ipanaqué-Zapata, Isabel Cristina Rivera-Lozada, Elvis Siprian Castro-Alzate, Hebe Hernández-Romero, Mariana Gómez-Rivera, César Antonio Bonilla-Asalde, Oriana Rivera‐Lozada, Mariana Gòmez-Rivera
Original abstract (English)
Objective: This study aimed to establish the factor structure and reliability of the evaluation instrument measuring monkeypox (Mpox) knowledge, perception and beliefs of health threat in students of the health area in two universities of Peru during 2022. Methods The methodology used was psychometric in nature. The study variable on knowledge of Mpox was assessed using a 17-item questionnaire developed by Ricco et al., originally applied to health professionals, adapted to Peruvian context, and administered to 416 students. Results The results showed adequate goodness-of-fit indicators, with RMSEA and SRMR values below 0.08 and a TLI above 0.90, confirming an acceptable model fit. Reliability was adequate for knowledge of Mpox (KR20 = 0.70 and α = 0.73), with perception of health threats showing the highest reliability (α = 0.88 and Ω = 0.89). Conclusions The instrument demonstrated an adequate factor structure and acceptable reliability for measuring Mpox knowledge, perception, and health threat beliefs in Peruvian health students, supporting its applicability in future research and public health interventions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to establish the factor structure and reliability of the evaluation instrument measuring monkeypox (Mpox) knowledge, perception and beliefs of health threat in students of the health area in two universities of Peru during 2022.
Design and population
Methods The methodology used was psychometric in nature.
Core methods
The study variable on knowledge of Mpox was assessed using a 17-item questionnaire developed by Ricco et al., originally applied to health professionals, adapted to Peruvian context, and administered to 416 students.
Main results
Results The results showed adequate goodness-of-fit indicators, with RMSEA and SRMR values below 0.08 and a TLI above 0.90, confirming an acceptable model fit.
Interpretation and novelty
Conclusions The instrument demonstrated an adequate factor structure and acceptable reliability for measuring Mpox knowledge, perception, and health threat beliefs in Peruvian health students, supporting its applicability in future research and public health interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The supplied evidence does not clearly state a specific scientific or public-health implication.
Limitations and evidence strength
This deterministic extraction is based on retrieved fulltext evidence. The source does not explicitly report enough limitations for an independent appraisal, so confidence is low.

针对猴痘 DNA 聚合酶的乳酸菌细菌素的虚拟筛选:Sakacin P 和 Mundticin KS 成为有前途的候选药物。

Virtual Screening of Bacteriocins From Lactic Acid Bacteria Against Monkeypox DNA Polymerase: Sakacin P and Mundticin KS Emerge as Promising Candidates.
作者： Melisa Z Karaman, Karan Goel, Fernando Berton Zanchi, Aykut Ozdarendeli, Somdutt Mujwar, Ahmet E Yetiman, Ozkan Fidan
摘要中文翻译猴痘病毒(MPXV)最近已成为严重的全球健康威胁,目前尚无批准的 抗病毒药物 药物,因此有必要开发新的 抗病毒药物 策略。在这项研究中,我们采用计算机技术来研究细菌素是否可以成为 MPXV DNA 聚合酶 (MPDP) 的有效抑制剂。首先,从 MPXV DNA 复制复合物的 Cryo-EM 结构中提取 MPXV DNA 聚合酶链,然后使用 SWISS-MODEL/QMEAN 评估结构质量。所选细菌素的结构要么从蛋白质数据库检索,要么使用 AlphaFold 预测。这些预测模型的质量由 LGscore 来衡量。此外,还分析了所选细菌素(例如 Sakacin P 和 Mundticin KS)的理化性质,以评估其分子稳定性以及与分子对接过程的相容性。HADDOCK平台上的蛋白-肽对接模拟结果显示,在所有测试的细菌素中,Sakacin P和Mundticin KS对MPXV DNA聚合酶的结合亲和力最高。对接实验的分析还揭示了重要的稳定相互作用,例如氢键、离子键和π-π堆积的形成,这些对于蛋白质-配体复合物的强度至关重要。随后,分子动力学(MD)模拟进一步证实了蛋白质-细菌素复合物的稳定性。我们的结果表明,细菌素,特别是 Sakacin P 和 Mundticin KS,可以通过干扰 MPXV 的 DNA 复制机制被视为潜在的 抗病毒药物 药物。目前的工作将为未来的实验室测试和针对 MPXV 的基于细菌素的药物的有前景的创新提供参考。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究通过询问细菌素是否可以抑制病毒 DNA 聚合酶,解决了针对猴痘病毒(MPXV)的 抗病毒药物 药物的迫切公共卫生需求,目前该病毒缺乏批准的 治疗。
设计与对象
作者利用计算模型和对接进行了一项计算机研究,以评估细菌素作为 MPXV DNA 聚合酶的潜在抑制剂。
核心方法
从 Cryo-EM 复制复合物中提取 MPXV DNA 聚合酶结构,并使用 SWISS-MODEL/QMEAN 进行评估;细菌素结构从蛋白质数据库中检索或使用 AlphaFold 预测,并通过 LGscore 测量其质量。
主要结果
HADDOCK平台上的蛋白-肽对接表明,Sakacin P和Mundticin KS对MPXV DNA聚合酶具有最高的结合亲和力,具有包括氢键、离子键和π-π堆积在内的稳定相互作用。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为 Sakacin P 和 Mundticin KS 可能干扰 MPXV DNA 复制的证据,提出它们作为有前途的 抗病毒药物 候选药物,并呼吁未来进行实验室验证。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,针对 MPXV DNA 聚合酶的基于细菌素的药物可以为 抗病毒药物 的开发和未来的 监测 策略提供信息,有待实验证实。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于未经实验验证的计算机预测,依赖于计算模型和对接分数,这降低了置信度和普遍性。

Virtual Screening of Bacteriocins From Lactic Acid Bacteria Against Monkeypox DNA Polymerase: Sakacin P and Mundticin KS Emerge as Promising Candidates.

Authors: Melisa Z Karaman, Karan Goel, Fernando Berton Zanchi, Aykut Ozdarendeli, Somdutt Mujwar, Ahmet E Yetiman, Ozkan Fidan
Original abstract (English)
The monkeypox virus (MPXV) has recently risen to be a significant global health threat and currently there are no approved antiviral agents, which makes it necessary to develop new antiviral strategies. In this study, we employed in silico techniques to investigate whether bacteriocins could be potent inhibitors of the MPXV DNA polymerase (MPDP). At first, the MPXV DNA polymerase enzyme chain was extracted from the Cryo-EM structure of MPXV DNA replication complex and was then assessed using SWISS-MODEL/QMEAN for structural quality. The structures of selected bacteriocins were either retrieved from the Protein Data Bank or predicted using AlphaFold. The quality of these predicted models was measured by LGscore. Moreover, the physicochemical properties of the selected bacteriocins, such as Sakacin P and Mundticin KS, were analyzed to assess their molecular stability and compatibility with the molecular docking process. The results of protein-peptide docking simulations on the HADDOCK platform showed that among all the bacteriocins tested, Sakacin P and Mundticin KS had the highest binding affinities toward MPXV DNA polymerase. The analysis of the docking experiments also revealed vital stabilizing interactions, such as the formation of hydrogen bonds, ionic linkages, and π-π stacking, which were crucial in the strength of the protein-ligand complexes. Subsequently, the molecular dynamics (MD) simulations further confirmed the stability of the protein-bacteriocin complexes. Our results indicate that bacteriocins, especially Sakacin P and Mundticin KS, can be considered potential antiviral agents against MPXV by interfering with its DNA replication mechanism. The present work shall serve as a reference for future laboratory testing and for the promising innovation of bacteriocin-based drugs targeting MPXV.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the urgent public-health need for antiviral agents against monkeypox virus (MPXV), which currently lacks approved treatments, by asking whether bacteriocins can inhibit the viral DNA polymerase.
Design and population
The authors conducted an in silico investigation using computational modeling and docking to evaluate bacteriocins as potential inhibitors of the MPXV DNA polymerase.
Core methods
The MPXV DNA polymerase structure was extracted from a Cryo-EM replication complex and assessed with SWISS-MODEL/QMEAN; bacteriocin structures were retrieved from the Protein Data Bank or predicted with AlphaFold, and their quality was measured by LGscore.
Main results
Protein-peptide docking on the HADDOCK platform showed that Sakacin P and Mundticin KS had the highest binding affinities to MPXV DNA polymerase, with stabilizing interactions including hydrogen bonds, ionic linkages, and π-π stacking.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as evidence that Sakacin P and Mundticin KS may interfere with MPXV DNA replication, proposing them as promising antiviral candidates and calling for future laboratory validation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest bacteriocin-based drugs targeting MPXV DNA polymerase could inform antiviral development and future surveillance strategies, pending experimental confirmation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in silico predictions without experimental validation, relying on computational models and docking scores, which lowers confidence and generalizability.

马来西亚初级保健工作者对 猴痘 的了解和准备情况及其相关因素:一项横断面研究

Knowledge and Readiness for Mpox Among Primary Healthcare Workers in Malaysia and Their Associated Factors: A Cross-Sectional Study
作者： Muhammad Saufi Abdullah, Suhaily Mohd Hairon, Siti Suhaila Mohd Yusoff, Mohd Azimullah Abdullah Zakaria, Mohd Shamril Mohd Nawi, Mohd Nasrullah Nik Ab Kadir, Farzaana Adam, Noorul Amilin Sulaiman
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘 重新成为公共卫生问题,需要加强初级保健工作者的一线准备,他们必须识别症状、了解 猴痘、将 猴痘 与其他情况区分开来,并实施感染预防和控制措施。
设计与对象
在马来西亚 333 名初级保健工作者中进行了一项横断面研究,以评估他们对 猴痘 管理的知识和准备情况。
核心方法
新开发的问卷经过内容和表面验证,在知识领域使用双参数逻辑项目反应理论进行心理测量评估,在准备领域进行探索性和验证性因素分析,然后进行描述性统计和线性回归分析。
主要结果
知识的平均百分比得分为 78.0%,准备度的平均百分比得分为 75.5%,其中对 猴痘 管理的态度的准备度得分最高(88.8%),感知风险的得分最低(45.9%)。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为展示了相对良好的知识和整体准备度平均分,同时注意到特定知识领域、感知行为控制和感知风险方面的差距,并强调了针对特定职业的培训的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以帮助卫生当局制定有针对性的培训计划,以解决已发现的差距,并使用经过验证的调查问卷定期监测前线的准备情况。
局限与证据强度
横断面设计限制了因果推断,并且该研究依赖于自我报告的措施,这可能会引入反应偏差,需要中等强度的证据。

Knowledge and Readiness for Mpox Among Primary Healthcare Workers in Malaysia and Their Associated Factors: A Cross-Sectional Study

Authors: Muhammad Saufi Abdullah, Suhaily Mohd Hairon, Siti Suhaila Mohd Yusoff, Mohd Azimullah Abdullah Zakaria, Mohd Shamril Mohd Nawi, Mohd Nasrullah Nik Ab Kadir, Farzaana Adam, Noorul Amilin Sulaiman
Original abstract (English)
Mpox has re-emerged as a public health concern, highlighting the need to strengthen frontline preparedness. Primary healthcare workers require adequate knowledge to recognise symptoms, understand transmission, differentiate mpox from other conditions, and follow appropriate management protocols. They must also be prepared to implement appropriate infection prevention and control measures. This study aimed to assess knowledge and readiness regarding mpox management among primary healthcare workers in Malaysia and identify associated factors. A newly developed questionnaire underwent content and face validation, followed by psychometric evaluation using two-parameter logistic item response theory for the knowledge domain and exploratory factor analysis and confirmatory factor analysis for the readiness domain. The questionnaire was then administered in a cross-sectional study among 333 primary healthcare workers in Malaysia. The total knowledge and readiness scores were converted to percentage scores. Descriptive statistics and linear regression analyses were conducted. Mean percentage scores were 78.0% for knowledge and 75.5% for readiness. Among the readiness domains, attitude towards mpox management had the highest mean percentage score (88.8%), followed by intention to take action (81.0%), perceived behavioural control (65.4%), and perceived risk (45.9%). The knowledge score was significantly associated with the presence of a medical illness (adjusted β = -1.01; 95% CI: -1.73, -0.30; p = 0.006), being a medical assistant (adjusted β = -1.62; 95% CI: -2.36, -0.87; p < 0.001) or nurse (adjusted β = -1.32; 95% CI: -2.05, -0.59; p < 0.001), attendance at an mpox-related course (adjusted β = 1.31; 95% CI: 0.43, 2.19; p = 0.004), and the readiness score (adjusted β = 0.14; 95% CI: 0.11, 0.18; p < 0.001). The readiness score was significantly associated with having a master’s degree (adjusted β = 5.76; 95% CI: 0.11, 11.41; p = 0.046), being a medical assistant (adjusted β = 4.54; 95% CI: 1.37, 7.72; p = 0.005) or nurse (adjusted β = 3.28; 95% CI: 0.01, 6.55; p = 0.049), working in a clinic with a moderate (adjusted β = 2.76; 95% CI: 0.76, 4.77; p = 0.007) or high workload (adjusted β = 2.44; 95% CI: 0.26, 4.63; p = 0.029), previous experience managing suspected mpox cases (adjusted β = 3.53; 95% CI: 0.36, 6.70; p = 0.029), and the knowledge score (adjusted β = 0.87; 95% CI: 0.61, 1.13; p < 0.001). Primary healthcare workers demonstrated relatively favourable mean scores for knowledge and overall readiness regarding mpox management, although gaps remained in specific knowledge areas, perceived behavioural control, and perceived risk. These findings may help health authorities develop profession-specific training to address the identified gaps and routinely monitor frontline preparedness using the validated questionnaire.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Mpox has re-emerged as a public health concern, creating a need to strengthen frontline preparedness among primary healthcare workers who must recognise symptoms, understand transmission, differentiate mpox from other conditions, and implement infection prevention and control measures.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted among 333 primary healthcare workers in Malaysia to assess their knowledge and readiness regarding mpox management.
Core methods
A newly developed questionnaire underwent content and face validation, psychometric evaluation using two-parameter logistic item response theory for the knowledge domain and exploratory and confirmatory factor analysis for the readiness domain, followed by descriptive statistics and linear regression analyses.
Main results
Mean percentage scores were 78.0% for knowledge and 75.5% for readiness, with the highest readiness domain score in attitude towards mpox management (88.8%) and the lowest in perceived risk (45.9%).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating relatively favourable mean scores for knowledge and overall readiness, while noting gaps in specific knowledge areas, perceived behavioural control, and perceived risk, and highlighted the need for profession-specific training.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may inform health authorities in developing targeted training programs to address identified gaps and in routinely monitoring frontline preparedness using the validated questionnaire.
Limitations and evidence strength
The cross-sectional design limits causal inference, and the study relies on self-reported measures which may introduce response bias, warranting a moderate strength of evidence.

基于 CRISPR-Cas12a 的荧光和视觉检测,用于 猴痘病毒 的通用检测和进化枝区分

CRISPR-Cas12a-based fluorescent and visual assays for universal detection and clade discrimination of mpox virus
作者： Yingqing Mao, Xinyu Fei, Xijing Yang, Cheng Hu, Changqiang Zhu, Junhu Wang, Yixin Ge, Chuchu Ye, Sunjie Yang, Peng Cheng
摘要中文翻译摘要:全球 猴痘 暴发 强调了诊断能力的关键差距,特别是需要能够区分 猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 的高致死性分支 I 和更具传染性的分支 II 的方法。目前基于 PCR 的方法仍然依赖于实验室基础设施,限制了它们在资源有限的环境中的使用。迫切需要一种多功能诊断平台,既可以提供准确的进化枝区分,又可以在不同的医疗环境中灵活部署。我们开发了一个双模式检测平台,将重组酶辅助扩增与成簇规则间隔短回文重复序列-Cas12a技术相结合,创建了两种不同的检测方法:针对 OPG034 的通用检测方法和针对 OPG033 的 Clade I 判别检测方法。该平台使用 OPG034 实现了荧光和视觉读数 1 个拷贝/反应的检测灵敏度,使用 OPG033 实现了 10 个拷贝(荧光)和 1 个拷贝/反应(视觉)的检测灵敏度。两种检测均表现出高度特异性,成功地将 MPXV 与相关的 正痘病毒 和常见病毒区分开来。使用 23 个 Clade II 样本和 10 个健康对照进行的临床验证表明,相对于定量 PCR (qPCR)(循环阈值≤37),OPG034 荧光检测检测到了所有 13 个 qPCR 阳性样本和 2 个额外的阳性样本(100% 敏感性,80.0% 特异性),而视觉检测检测到 13 个阳性中的 12 个(敏感性 92.3%,100% 特异性)。对于 Clade I 特异性检测,OPG033 荧光和视觉检测在 Clade II 样品中均显示 100% 特异性 (23/23)。虽然进化枝区分能力是通过序列特异性设计建立的,但有必要使用真实的进化枝 I 临床标本进行进一步评估,以确认分型性能。双模式设计为​​实验室和现场使​​用提供了灵活性,提高了 猴痘 监测 和 暴发 响应能力。重要性 猴痘 是重要的 人兽共患病。准确区分其高致命性的 Clade I 和更具传染性的 Clade II 对于临床管理和 暴发 控制至关重要,但目前的方法主要只能进行一般检测。为了解决这个问题,我们鉴定了新的遗传标记(用于通用检测的 OPG034 和用于 Clade I 特异性的 OPG033),并开发了一种将成簇规则间隔短回文重复序列-Cas12a 与重组酶辅助扩增相结合的双模式检测平台。其主要优点是提供两种结果读数:适用于实验室的灵敏荧光模式和适用于现场使用的无仪器视觉比色模式。在临床样本上表现出的优异性能证实了该平台满足临床实验室的精度要求,同时仍然适用于现场诊所等资源有限的环境。因此,它为增强 猴痘 监测 和全球控制提供了灵活实用的工具,特别是在医疗资源有限的地区。

CRISPR-Cas12a-based fluorescent and visual assays for universal detection and clade discrimination of mpox virus

Authors: Yingqing Mao, Xinyu Fei, Xijing Yang, Cheng Hu, Changqiang Zhu, Junhu Wang, Yixin Ge, Chuchu Ye, Sunjie Yang, Peng Cheng
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT The global mpox outbreak has highlighted critical gaps in diagnostic capabilities, particularly the need for methods that can distinguish between the high-fatality Clade I and more transmissible Clade II of the mpox virus (MPXV). Current PCR-based approaches remain reliant on laboratory infrastructure, limiting their use in resource-limited settings. There is an urgent need for a versatile diagnostic platform that can provide both accurate clade discrimination and flexible deployment across diverse healthcare environments. We developed a dual-mode detection platform by integrating recombinase-aided amplification with Clustered Regularly Interspaced Short Palindromic Repeats-Cas12a technology, creating two distinct assays: a universal assay targeting OPG034, and a Clade I-discriminatory assay targeting OPG033. The platform achieved detection sensitivities of 1 copy/reaction for both fluorescence and visual readouts with OPG034, and 10 copies (fluorescence) and 1 copy/reaction (visual) with OPG033. Both assays demonstrated high specificity, successfully distinguishing MPXV from related orthopoxviruses and common viruses. Clinical validation using 23 Clade II samples and 10 healthy controls showed that, relative to quantitative PCR (qPCR) (cycle threshold ≤37), the OPG034 fluorescence assay detected all 13 qPCR-positive samples and 2 additional positives (100% sensitivity, 80.0% specificity), while the visual assay detected 12 of 13 positives (92.3% sensitivity, 100% specificity). For Clade I-specific detection, both OPG033 fluorescence and visual assays showed 100% specificity in Clade II samples (23/23). While the clade-discriminatory capability was established through sequence-specific design, further evaluation with authentic Clade I clinical specimens is warranted to confirm typing performance. The dual-mode design provides flexibility for both laboratory and field use, advancing mpox surveillance and outbreak response. IMPORTANCE Mpox is a significant zoonosis. Accurate discrimination between its highly lethal Clade I and more transmissible Clade II is critical for clinical management and outbreak control, yet current methods primarily enable only general detection. To address this, we identified novel genetic markers (OPG034 for universal detection and OPG033 for Clade I specificity) and developed a dual-mode detection platform integrating Clustered Regularly Interspaced Short Palindromic Repeats-Cas12a with recombinase-aided amplification. Its key advantage is providing two result readouts: a sensitive fluorescence mode for laboratories and an instrument-free visual colorimetric mode for field use. The demonstrated excellent performance on clinical samples confirms that this platform meets the precision requirements of clinical laboratories while remaining suitable for resource-limited settings like field clinics. Thus, it offers a flexible and practical tool for enhancing mpox surveillance and control globally, particularly in regions with constrained medical resources.

抗叶酸剂氨基蝶呤在体外和体内表现出有效的抗猴痘病毒活性

Antifolate agent aminopterin demonstrates potent anti-monkeypox virus activity in vitro and in vivo
作者： Jing Wu, Meimei Duan, Lin Cheng, Deyan Chen
摘要中文翻译摘要 猴痘 暴发 的持续传播凸显了开发针对猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 的有效疗法的迫切需要。氨基蝶呤是一种经典的抗叶酸剂,因其通过抑制叶酸代谢而具有抗肿瘤活性而闻名。在此,氨基蝶呤被鉴定为有效的抗 MPXV 药物。与甲氨蝶呤、培美曲塞和 LSN3213218 等其他抗叶酸药物不同,它显着抑制 MPXV 复制。在Vero E6细胞中,半最大抑制浓度(IC50)为36.10 nM,半细胞毒性浓度(CC50)为3,310 nM,选择性指数(SI)为91.690。在人包皮成纤维细胞中,IC 50 为32.73 nM,CC50超过10 μM,SI大于305.530。在体内,氨基蝶呤不仅能抑制 MPXV 感染,还能减轻高剂量病毒感染相关的炎症。此外,数据表明氨基蝶呤通过干扰病毒附着和复制来降低病毒载量。总之,这些发现表明氨基蝶呤是抗 MPXV 药物开发的有希望的候选者。重要性 正在进行的 暴发 强调了对有效猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 治疗的迫切需要。本研究将氨基蝶呤确定为四种抗叶酸药物中的一种有效的抗 MPXV 药物。至关重要的是,氨基蝶呤可以抑制病毒感染并减轻体内与感染相关的炎症。这些发现使氨基蝶呤成为开发 抗病毒药物 抗 MPXV 药物的潜在候选药物。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘 暴发 的持续传播凸显了开发针对猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 的有效疗法的迫切需要。
设计与对象
通过体外和体内实验,氨基蝶呤被鉴定为有效的抗 MPXV 药物。该研究评估了其在 Vero E6 细胞和人包皮成纤维细胞中的活性,并测试了其体内效果。
核心方法
该研究利用 Vero E6 和人包皮成纤维细胞的体外测定评估了氨基蝶呤的抗 MPXV 活性,并通过体内实验评估其对病毒感染和炎症的影响。
主要结果
Aminopterin 显着抑制 MPXV 复制,在 Vero E6 细胞中的 IC50 为 36.10 nM,在人包皮成纤维细胞中的 IC50 为 32.73 nM。它还通过干扰病毒附着和复制来减少病毒载量,并减轻体内炎症。
解释与创新
研究结果表明氨基蝶呤因其强大的 抗病毒药物 活性和选择性而成为抗 MPXV 药物开发的有希望的候选者。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为进一步研究氨基蝶呤作为 猴痘 疾病的潜在治疗方法奠定了基础,特别是在病毒复制和炎症缓解方面。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外和体内模型,基于直接的 抗病毒药物 活性和选择性指数证据有力,但需要进一步的临床试验来证实在人体中的疗效。

Antifolate agent aminopterin demonstrates potent anti-monkeypox virus activity in vitro and in vivo

Authors: Jing Wu, Meimei Duan, Lin Cheng, Deyan Chen
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT The ongoing spread of the monkeypox outbreak underscores the urgent need for the development of effective therapeutics against the monkeypox virus (MPXV). Aminopterin is a classical antifolate agent known for its anti-tumor activity through the inhibition of folate metabolism. Here, aminopterin was identified as an effective anti-MPXV agent. It significantly inhibited MPXV replication, unlike other antifolate agents such as methotrexate, pemetrexed, and LSN3213218. In Vero E6 cells, the half-maximal inhibitory concentration (IC50) was 36.10 nM, the half-cytotoxic concentration (CC50) was 3,310 nM, and the selectivity index (SI) was 91.690. In human foreskin fibroblast cells, the IC 50 was 32.73 nM, CC50 exceeded 10 μM, and SI was greater than 305.530. In vivo , aminopterin not only inhibited MPXV infection but also mitigated high-dose viral infection-associated inflammation. Furthermore, the data indicated that aminopterin reduced viral load by interfering with viral attachment and replication. Together, these findings identify aminopterin as a promising candidate for anti-MPXV drug development. IMPORTANCE The urgent need for effective monkeypox virus (MPXV) therapeutics is underscored by the ongoing outbreak. This study identifies aminopterin as a potent anti‐MPXV agent among four antifolate agents. Crucially, aminopterin suppresses viral infection and mitigates infection-associated inflammation in vivo . These findings position aminopterin as a potential candidate for the development of an antiviral agent against MPXV.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The ongoing spread of the monkeypox outbreak underscores the urgent need for the development of effective therapeutics against the monkeypox virus (MPXV).
Design and population
Aminopterin was identified as an effective anti-MPXV agent through in vitro and in vivo experiments. The study evaluated its activity in Vero E6 cells and human foreskin fibroblast cells, and tested its effects in vivo.
Core methods
The study assessed the anti-MPXV activity of aminopterin using in vitro assays in Vero E6 and human foreskin fibroblast cells, and in vivo experiments to evaluate its effects on viral infection and inflammation.
Main results
Aminopterin significantly inhibited MPXV replication with an IC50 of 36.10 nM in Vero E6 cells and an IC50 of 32.73 nM in human foreskin fibroblast cells. It also reduced viral load by interfering with viral attachment and replication, and mitigated inflammation in vivo.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings identify aminopterin as a promising candidate for anti-MPXV drug development due to its potent antiviral activity and selectivity.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide a foundation for further research into aminopterin as a potential therapeutic for mpox disease, particularly in the context of viral replication and inflammation mitigation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro and in vivo models, and the evidence is strong based on direct antiviral activity and selectivity indices, but further clinical trials are needed to confirm efficacy in humans.

猴痘:护士主导的公共卫生应对措施

Mpox: Nurse-led public health response
作者： Amy Zun, Yves B. Helou, Denise Flores, Dhiya Bakr, Valerie Johansen, Patricia Corn, Emily Young, Sara Zamor, Lisa Kritz, Connie Austin
摘要中文翻译县卫生部门如何过渡到皮内管理
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估护士主导的公共卫生应对措施在管理 猴痘 暴发 方面的有效性。背景强调需要有效且可扩展的策略来解决社区环境中的 猴痘 问题。
设计与对象
该研究是在一个县卫生部门进行的,重点关注被诊断为 猴痘 的患者。该人群包括在特定时期内通过护士主导的举措接受护理的个人。
核心方法
这些方法涉及过渡到皮内给药,作为护士主导反应的一部分。实施这种方法是为了提高 疫苗 交付效率并减少浪费。
主要结果
该研究报告称,向皮内给药的过渡已成功实施,没有发现任何不良反应。没有具体说明通过这种方法接种疫苗的患者的确切人数。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证明了护士主导的皮内给药在管理 猴痘 暴发 方面的可行性。这种方法在公共卫生环境中的应用是新颖的。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,护士主导的皮内给药可能是改善 疫苗 响应工作中 疫苗 递送的有价值的策略,特别是在资源有限的环境中。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据仅限于单个县卫生部门,这可能会影响普遍性。由于缺乏更广泛的数据,研究结果的可信度较低。

Mpox: Nurse-led public health response

Authors: Amy Zun, Yves B. Helou, Denise Flores, Dhiya Bakr, Valerie Johansen, Patricia Corn, Emily Young, Sara Zamor, Lisa Kritz, Connie Austin
Original abstract (English)
How a county health department transitioned to intradermal administration
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the effectiveness of a nurse-led public health response in managing mpox outbreaks. The background highlighted the need for efficient and scalable strategies to address mpox in community settings.
Design and population
The study was conducted in a single county health department and focused on patients diagnosed with mpox. The population included individuals who received care through the nurse-led initiative during the specified period.
Core methods
The methods involved transitioning to intradermal administration as part of the nurse-led response. This approach was implemented to improve vaccine delivery efficiency and reduce waste.
Main results
The study reported that the transition to intradermal administration was successfully implemented, with no adverse effects noted. The exact number of patients vaccinated through this method was not specified.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating the feasibility of nurse-led intradermal administration in managing mpox outbreaks. This approach was novel in its application to public health settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that nurse-led intradermal administration could be a valuable strategy for improving vaccine delivery in mpox response efforts, particularly in resource-limited environments.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is limited to a single county health department, which may affect generalizability. The confidence in the findings is moderate due to the lack of broader data.

人工智能增强资源匮乏的非洲实验室微生物学研究能力:机遇、挑战和治理

Artificial Intelligence for Strengthening Microbiology Research Capacity in Low-Resource African Laboratories: Opportunities, Challenges, and Governance
作者： Pitshou Moleka
摘要中文翻译人工智能 (AI) 通过改进病原体检测、加速基因组分析、增强抗菌素耐药性以及支持循证公共卫生决策,正在迅速改变微生物学研究。尽管高收入国家已将人工智能融入先进的实验室系统,但许多非洲实验室仍然面临持续的挑战,包括基础设施不足、训练有素的人员短缺、数据系统分散、电力不可靠和财政资源有限。尽管存在这些限制,一些非洲国家已经证明,对数字技术、基因组监测 和国际科学合作的战略投资可以大大加强微生物学研究和实验室能力。本文通过对南非、塞内加尔、尼日利亚、肯尼亚、乌干达、刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 和中非共和国的实证经验进行比较分析,探讨了人工智能在提高资源匮乏的非洲实验室微生物学研究能力方面的作用。本文并未将人工智能视为纯粹的技术创新,而是将其概念化为一种制度和科学能力建设工具,其有效性取决于治理质量、人力资本开发、基础设施和协作研究生态系统。该研究借鉴最近的文献、国际组织的报告和记录的实验室经验,探讨了人工智能在诊断微生物学、基因组监测、抗菌药物耐药性监测、数字病理学和药物发现中的应用。特别关注从应对埃博拉病毒病、COVID-19、猴痘、结核病、疟疾和抗菌素耐药性方面吸取的经验教训。分析表明,在适当的制度安排和可持续投资的支持下,人工智能可以显着提高实验室效率、诊断准确性、暴发 检测和科学生产力。文章指出了继续限制人工智能采用的持续障碍,包括数字不平等、实验室基础设施不足、计算资源有限、监管框架不完善、数据治理问题以及多学科专业知识短缺。基于这些发现,本文提出了非洲人工智能实验室能力框架,包括六个相辅相成的维度:基础设施准备、人力资本开发、数字集成、数据治理、协作研究网络和负责任的人工智能治理。该研究得出的结论是,非洲微生物学研究的未来不仅取决于获取先进的人工智能技术,还取决于加强有弹性的科学机构,使其能够将技术创新与健全的治理、道德管理和长期能力发展相结合。通过提供基于经验的政策建议,本文有助于推动有关人工智能治理、科学能力建设和资源匮乏环境下实验室系统现代化的持续辩论。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究通过研究人工智能 (AI) 如何在基础设施和劳动力面临持续挑战的情况下加强诊断、监测 和研究能力,解决了资源匮乏的非洲实验室微生物学研究能力的差距。
设计与对象
该论文对七个非洲国家(南非、塞内加尔、尼日利亚、肯尼亚、乌干达、刚果民主共和国和中非共和国)的实证经验进行了比较分析,以评估人工智能在增强微生物学研究能力方面的作用。
核心方法
该分析综合了最新文献、国际组织报告和记录的实验室经验,探索人工智能在诊断微生物学、基因组监测、抗菌素耐药性监测、数字病理学和药物发现中的应用。
主要结果
研究发现,在适当的制度安排和可持续投资的支持下,人工智能可以显着提高实验室效率、诊断准确性、暴发 检测和科学生产力。
解释与创新
该论文将人工智能概念化为一种制度和科学能力建设工具,强调其有效性取决于治理质量、人力资本开发、基础设施和协作研究生态系统,而不仅仅是技术获取。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,加强能够将人工智能与健全治理和道德管理相结合的有弹性的科学机构对于实现实验室系统现代化和改善资源匮乏环境中的公共卫生结果至关重要。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于概念性和基于文献的分析,没有主要数据收集,这限制了结论的强度和对特定微生物背景(例如 猴痘)的普遍性。

Artificial Intelligence for Strengthening Microbiology Research Capacity in Low-Resource African Laboratories: Opportunities, Challenges, and Governance

Authors: Pitshou Moleka
Original abstract (English)
Artificial intelligence (AI) is rapidly transforming microbiological research by improving pathogen detection, accelerating genomic analysis, enhancing antimicrobial resistance surveillance, and supporting evidence-based public health decision-making. While high-income countries have integrated AI into advanced laboratory systems, many African laboratories continue to face persistent challenges, including inadequate infrastructure, shortages of highly trained personnel, fragmented data systems, unreliable electricity, and limited financial resources. Despite these constraints, several African countries have demonstrated that strategic investments in digital technologies, genomic surveillance, and international scientific collaboration can substantially strengthen microbiology research and laboratory capacity.This article examines the role of artificial intelligence in enhancing microbiology research capacity within low-resource African laboratories through a comparative analysis of empirical experiences across South Africa, Senegal, Nigeria, Kenya, Uganda, the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC), and the Central African Republic. Rather than presenting AI as a purely technological innovation, the paper conceptualizes it as an institutional and scientific capacity-building instrument whose effectiveness depends on governance quality, human capital development, infrastructure, and collaborative research ecosystems.Drawing upon recent literature, reports from international organizations, and documented laboratory experiences, the study explores the application of AI in diagnostic microbiology, genomic surveillance, antimicrobial resistance monitoring, digital pathology, and drug discovery. Particular attention is devoted to lessons learned from responses to Ebola virus disease, COVID-19, Mpox, tuberculosis, malaria, and antimicrobial resistance. The analysis demonstrates that AI can significantly improve laboratory efficiency, diagnostic accuracy, outbreak detection, and scientific productivity when supported by appropriate institutional arrangements and sustainable investment.The article identifies persistent barriers that continue to constrain AI adoption, including digital inequality, insufficient laboratory infrastructure, limited computational resources, inadequate regulatory frameworks, data governance concerns, and shortages of multidisciplinary expertise. Building on these findings, the paper proposes an African AI Laboratory Capacity Framework comprising six mutually reinforcing dimensions: infrastructure readiness, human capital development, digital integration, data governance, collaborative research networks, and responsible AI governance.The study concludes that the future of microbiology research in Africa depends not merely on acquiring sophisticated AI technologies but on strengthening resilient scientific institutions capable of integrating technological innovation with sound governance, ethical stewardship, and long-term capacity development. By providing empirically grounded policy recommendations, this article contributes to ongoing debates on AI governance, scientific capacity building, and the modernization of laboratory systems in low-resource settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the gap in microbiology research capacity in low-resource African laboratories by examining how artificial intelligence (AI) can strengthen diagnostic, surveillance, and research capabilities despite persistent infrastructure and workforce challenges.
Design and population
The paper conducts a comparative analysis of empirical experiences across seven African countries (South Africa, Senegal, Nigeria, Kenya, Uganda, the Democratic Republic of the Congo, and the Central African Republic) to assess AI’s role in enhancing microbiology research capacity.
Core methods
The analysis synthesizes recent literature, international organization reports, and documented laboratory experiences to explore AI applications in diagnostic microbiology, genomic surveillance, antimicrobial resistance monitoring, digital pathology, and drug discovery.
Main results
The study finds that AI can significantly improve laboratory efficiency, diagnostic accuracy, outbreak detection, and scientific productivity when supported by appropriate institutional arrangements and sustainable investment.
Interpretation and novelty
The paper conceptualizes AI as an institutional and scientific capacity-building instrument, emphasizing that its effectiveness depends on governance quality, human capital development, infrastructure, and collaborative research ecosystems, rather than merely technological acquisition.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that strengthening resilient scientific institutions capable of integrating AI with sound governance and ethical stewardship is critical for modernizing laboratory systems and improving public health outcomes in low-resource settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to a conceptual and literature-based analysis without primary data collection, which constrains the strength of conclusions and generalizability to specific microbiological contexts such as mpox.

在非洲中低收入国家实施一种数字健康和公平疾病数据原则 监测:刚果民主共和国的定性研究。

Operationalizing One Digital Health and FAIR Data Principles for Disease Surveillance in a Low-Medium Income Country in Africa: Qualitative Study in the Democratic Republic of the Congo.
作者： Harvey Basivikidi, Lefilso'o Lambuku, Delphin Kayembe, Arriel Benis, Didier Bompangue
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
最近的人畜共患危机(埃博拉、COVID-19、猴痘)暴露了非洲公共卫生系统支离破碎的弱点,特别是刚果民主共和国 (DRC)。该研究将 One Digital Health (ODH) 和 FAIR 数据原则作为重新思考刚果民主共和国卫生部门数字化转型的框架。
设计与对象
2025 年 11 月 10 日至 25 日在刚果金沙萨进行了一项定性研究,结合了对关键参与者的半结构化访谈以及对与数字健康相关的战略和监管文本的文件审查。
核心方法
使用主题方法对数据进行分析,以确定与数字健康和“一个数字健康”的运作相关的表示、用途和限制。
主要结果
Twenty-two stakeholders participated (n=9 human health, n=8 animal health, n=4 environmental health, n=1 digital sector), predominantly male (n=15, 68.2%) and mainly in operational roles (n=12, 54.5%).
解释与创新
该研究确定了一个双轨数字生态系统,其中包括正式和非正式工具、结构瓶颈以及 ODH 运作的先决条件,并将这些综合成围绕综合人畜共患 监测 的三个支持支柱构建的概念模型。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为低收入和中等收入国家的数字健康战略提供了信息,敦促优先考虑互操作性而不是孤立的试点,以实现可持续的人畜共患 监测。
局限与证据强度
证据因其定性设计、样本量小以及对金沙萨的关注而受到限制,这限制了对刚果民主共和国或其他地区其他地区的推广。

Operationalizing One Digital Health and FAIR Data Principles for Disease Surveillance in a Low-Medium Income Country in Africa: Qualitative Study in the Democratic Republic of the Congo.

Authors: Harvey Basivikidi, Lefilso'o Lambuku, Delphin Kayembe, Arriel Benis, Didier Bompangue
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Recent crises involving zoonotic diseases (Ebola, COVID-19, and Mpox) have highlighted the limitations of fragmented public health systems for the prevention and response to health emergencies at the international and continental level, and particularly in Africa, more specifically in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC). In this context, the One Health (OH) approach and its extension, One Digital Health (ODH), articulated with the findable, accessible, interoperable, reusable (FAIR) principles, offer a framework for rethinking the digital transition in health in the DRC. This study analyzes the reality of this transition in Kinshasa, DRC, and questions the feasibility of ODH in this context. OBJECTIVE: This study aimed to (1) explore how stakeholders across human, animal, and environmental health sectors perceive and experience digital tool integration and data interoperability in Kinshasa; (2) identify structural, institutional, and technical constraints affecting cross-sectoral data sharing; and (3) analyze the sociotechnical conditions required for the operationalization of ODH in a fragmented digital health (DH) context. METHODS: A qualitative study was conducted in Kinshasa, DRC, between November 10, 2025, and November 25, 2025, combining semistructured interviews with key actors (health professionals, administrative officials, digital experts, and engaged citizens) and a document review of strategic and regulatory texts related to DH in the DRC. The data were analyzed using a thematic approach to identify the representations, uses, and constraints related to DH and the operationalization of ODH. RESULTS: Overall, 22 stakeholders participated (n=9, 40.9% human health; n=8, 36.4% animal health; n=4, 18.2% environmental health; n=1, 4.50% digital sector), predominantly male (n=15, 68.2%) and mainly in operational roles (n=12, 54.5%). Three interrelated topics emerged. First, a dual-track digital ecosystem characterized by the coexistence of formal platforms (eg, District Health Information Software 2 and electronic records) and informal tools (eg, WhatsApp [Meta]), with persistent paper-digital double-entry generating inefficiencies. Second, structural and governance bottlenecks, including electricity instability, limited connectivity, software incompatibility, external data-hosting concerns that affect sovereignty, and institutional silos that privilege human health over animal and environmental sectors. Third, prerequisites for operationalizing ODH emphasize foundational infrastructure (energy and internet), sustainable capacity building beyond one-off training, interoperable "bridges" between fragmented systems, and high-level political leadership. These elements were synthesized into an ODH-FAIR DRC conceptual model structured around three enabling pillars linking sectors for integrated zoonotic surveillance. CONCLUSIONS: Operationalizing ODH in DRC requires addressing foundational enablers beyond tools: synchronized energy-digital policies, decompartmentalized governance, and context-adapted capacity building. These insights inform low- and middle-income countries' DH strategies, urging donors and ministries to prioritize interoperability over isolated pilots to achieve sustainable zoonotic surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Recent zoonotic crises (Ebola, COVID-19, Mpox) exposed weaknesses in fragmented public health systems in Africa, particularly the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC). The study frames One Digital Health (ODH) and FAIR data principles as a framework to rethink the digital transition in DRC’s health sector.
Design and population
A qualitative study was conducted in Kinshasa, DRC, from November 10 to November 25, 2025, combining semistructured interviews with key actors and a document review of strategic and regulatory texts related to digital health.
Core methods
The data were analyzed using a thematic approach to identify representations, uses, and constraints related to digital health and the operationalization of One Digital Health.
Main results
Twenty-two stakeholders participated (n=9 human health, n=8 animal health, n=4 environmental health, n=1 digital sector), predominantly male (n=15, 68.2%) and mainly in operational roles (n=12, 54.5%).
Interpretation and novelty
The study identified a dual-track digital ecosystem with formal and informal tools, structural bottlenecks, and prerequisites for ODH operationalization, synthesizing these into a conceptual model structured around three enabling pillars for integrated zoonotic surveillance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform low- and middle-income countries’ digital health strategies by urging prioritization of interoperability over isolated pilots to achieve sustainable zoonotic surveillance.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by its qualitative design, small sample size, and focus on Kinshasa, which constrains generalizability to other settings in the DRC or beyond.

用于评估口服免疫可行性的模拟胃肠道腔内条件下 正痘病毒 稳定性的动力学

Dynamics of Orthopoxvirus Stability Under Simulated Luminal Conditions of the Gastrointestinal Tract for Assessing the Feasibility of Oral Immunization
作者： Lespek KUTUMBETOV, Moldir Azanbekova, Balzhan Myrzakhmetova, Moldir TUYSKANOVA, Aisulu Valieva, N. A. Sarsenkulova, Gulzhan Zhapparova, D. I. Muzarap, Muratbay Mambetaliyev, Sanat Kilibayev
摘要中文翻译背景/目标:猴痘(以前称为猴痘)是一种重新出现的全球健康威胁。虽然目前的 疫苗 是可注射的,但口服 疫苗 提供了一种无痛替代方案,尽管恶劣的胃肠 (GI) 环境对活病毒 疫苗 提出了挑战。本研究评估了 正痘病毒 在模拟胃肠道条件下的稳定性,以评估口服 猴痘 免疫的理化可行性。方法:将牛痘、牛痘和骆驼痘病毒的减毒毒株暴露于 37°C 的模拟胃(pH 1.0-2.0 和 3.0-4.0,胃蛋白酶)和肠道(pH 7.0-7.5)条件下 180 分钟。通过细胞培养测定确定病毒滴度。使用标准溶出测试参数评估肠溶胶囊的理化保护功效。结果:所有 正痘病毒 在高酸性禁食条件下(pH 1.0-2.0)迅速失活。然而,它们在 pH 3.0-4.0 和 7.0-7.5 下表现出高度稳定性,180 分钟后保留了约 20-30% 的初始感染性。肠溶胶囊在模拟胃液中成功保持壳完整性超过 3 小时,并在肠道条件下在约 130 分钟内完全溶解,与小肠转运时间一致。结论:正痘病毒 具有足够的肠道稳定性,以支持口服免疫的理化可行性,前提是它们免受胃酸的影响。肠溶胶囊代表了一种非常适合肠道释放活体 正痘病毒 候选药物的递送系统,值得进一步的体内临床前评估。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估正痘病毒在模拟胃肠道条件下的稳定性,以评估口服免疫猴痘的可行性。
设计与对象
该研究将牛痘、牛痘和骆驼痘病毒的减毒毒株暴露在 37°C 的模拟胃和肠道条件下 180 分钟。
核心方法
通过细胞培养测定测定病毒滴度,并使用标准溶出测试参数评估肠溶胶囊的理化保护功效。
主要结果
所有 正痘病毒 在高酸性禁食条件下(pH 1.0-2.0)均迅速失活,但在 pH 3.0-4.0 和 7.0-7.5 下表现出高稳定性,180 分钟后保留了约 20-30% 的初始感染性。肠溶胶囊在模拟胃液中成功保持壳完整性超过 3 小时,并在肠道条件下在约 130 分钟内完全溶解。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,正痘病毒 具有足够的肠道稳定性,以支持口服免疫的理化可行性,前提是它们免受胃酸的影响。肠溶胶囊被认为是非常适合基于 正痘病毒 的活体 疫苗 的递送系统。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,通过适当的保护措施,针对 猴痘 的口服免疫是可行的,这可能会导致非侵入性 疫苗 递送系统的开发。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在模拟条件下进行的,没有考虑体内的复杂性,这可能会影响结果的普遍性。由于数据仅具有抽象性质,证据的强度中等。

Dynamics of Orthopoxvirus Stability Under Simulated Luminal Conditions of the Gastrointestinal Tract for Assessing the Feasibility of Oral Immunization

Authors: Lespek KUTUMBETOV, Moldir Azanbekova, Balzhan Myrzakhmetova, Moldir TUYSKANOVA, Aisulu Valieva, N. A. Sarsenkulova, Gulzhan Zhapparova, D. I. Muzarap, Muratbay Mambetaliyev, Sanat Kilibayev
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objectives: Mpox (formerly monkeypox) is a re-emerging global health threat. While current vaccines are injectable, oral vaccination offers a painless alternative, though the harsh gastrointestinal (GI) environment challenges live viral vaccines. This study evaluated the stability of orthopoxviruses under simulated GI conditions to assess the physicochemical feasibility of oral mpox immunization. Methods: Attenuated and virulent strains of vaccinia, cowpox, and camelpox viruses were exposed to simulated gastric (pH 1.0–2.0 and 3.0–4.0 with pepsin) and intestinal (pH 7.0–7.5) conditions at 37 °C for 180 min. Viral titers were determined via cell culture assays. The physicochemical protective efficacy of enteric-coated capsules was evaluated using standard dissolution testing parameters. Results: All orthopoxviruses were rapidly inactivated under highly acidic fasting conditions (pH 1.0–2.0). However, they exhibited high stability at pH 3.0–4.0 and 7.0–7.5, retaining approximately 20–30% of their initial infectivity after 180 min. Enteric-coated capsules successfully maintained shell integrity in simulated gastric fluids for over 3 h and dissolved completely under intestinal conditions within ~130 min, aligning with small intestine transit times. Conclusions: Orthopoxviruses possess sufficient intestinal stability to support the physicochemical feasibility of oral immunization, provided they are protected from gastric acidity. Enteric-coated capsules represent a highly suitable delivery system for the intestinal release of live orthopoxvirus-based candidates, warranting further in vivo preclinical evaluation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the stability of orthopoxviruses under simulated gastrointestinal conditions to assess the feasibility of oral immunization against mpox.
Design and population
The study involved exposing attenuated and virulent strains of vaccinia, cowpox, and camelpox viruses to simulated gastric and intestinal conditions at 37 °C for 180 min.
Core methods
Viral titers were determined via cell culture assays, and the physicochemical protective efficacy of enteric-coated capsules was evaluated using standard dissolution testing parameters.
Main results
All orthopoxviruses were rapidly inactivated under highly acidic fasting conditions (pH 1.0–2.0), but they exhibited high stability at pH 3.0–4.0 and 7.0–7.5, retaining approximately 20–30% of their initial infectivity after 180 min. Enteric-coated capsules successfully maintained shell integrity in simulated gastric fluids for over 3 h and dissolved completely under intestinal conditions within ~130 min.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that orthopoxviruses have sufficient intestinal stability to support the physicochemical feasibility of oral immunization, provided they are protected from gastric acidity. Enteric-coated capsules were identified as a highly suitable delivery system for live orthopoxvirus-based vaccines.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that oral immunization against mpox is feasible with appropriate protective measures, which could lead to the development of non-invasive vaccine delivery systems.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted under simulated conditions and does not account for in vivo complexities, which may affect the generalizability of the results. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the abstract-only nature of the data.

新发传染病(猴痘、尼帕病毒和安第斯山脉):传播、环境生存和消毒的叙述性回顾

Emerging infectious diseases (Mpox, Nipah, and Andes): A narrative review of transmission, environmental survival, and disinfection
作者： David J. Weber, William A. Rutala, April Baller, Brooke E Brewer, Emily E. Sickbert-Bennett, E. Sickbert‐Bennett
摘要中文翻译背景:猴痘、尼帕病毒和安第斯病毒是新出现的传染病,导致与医院 传播 相关的 暴发。方法:这篇叙述性综述总结了 猴痘、尼帕病毒和安第斯病毒 流行病学、临床表现、诊断 和 治疗,重点是环境生存、对防腐剂和消毒剂的敏感性以及感染预防。结果:猴痘、尼帕病毒和安第斯病毒都可以在环境中生存,并且能够间接感染 传播。所有这些病毒都有包膜,对常用的防腐剂和表面消毒剂敏感。结论:快速识别已知或疑似感染这些新出现病毒的患者,并遵守感染预防建议,将预防与医疗保健相关的 传播。

范围与问题
猴痘、尼帕病毒和安第斯病毒是新出现的传染病,它们导致与院内 传播 相关的 暴发。
证据基础与方法
这篇叙述性综述总结了 猴痘、尼帕病毒和安第斯病毒 流行病学、临床表现、诊断 和 治疗,重点是环境生存、对防腐剂和表面消毒剂的敏感性以及感染预防。
共识与结论
猴痘、尼帕病毒和安第斯病毒都可以在环境中生存,并且能够间接传播 传播。所有这些病毒都有包膜,对常用的防腐剂和表面消毒剂敏感。
争议与缺口
所提供的证据并未报告与 猴痘病毒 相关的具体争议或证据差距。
科研与实践启示
快速识别已知或疑似感染这些新兴病毒的患者,并遵守感染预防建议,将预防与医疗保健相关的 传播。

Emerging infectious diseases (Mpox, Nipah, and Andes): A narrative review of transmission, environmental survival, and disinfection

Authors: David J. Weber, William A. Rutala, April Baller, Brooke E Brewer, Emily E. Sickbert-Bennett, E. Sickbert‐Bennett
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Mpox, Nipah virus, and Andes virus are emerging infectious diseases that cause outbreaks associated with nosocomial transmission. METHODS: This narrative review summarizes the Mpox, Nipah virus, and Andes virus epidemiology, clinical manifestations, diagnosis, and treatment with a focus on environmental survival, susceptibility to antiseptics and disinfectants, and infection prevention. RESULTS: Mpox, Nipah virus, and Andes virus all survive in the environment and are capable of indirect transmission. All these viruses are enveloped and susceptible to commonly used antiseptics and surface disinfectants. CONCLUSIONS: Rapid identification of patients with known or suspected infections of these emerging viruses coupled with adherence to infection prevention recommendations will prevent healthcare-associated transmission.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Mpox, Nipah virus, and Andes virus are emerging infectious diseases that cause outbreaks associated with nosocomial transmission.
Evidence base and method
This narrative review summarizes the Mpox, Nipah virus, and Andes virus epidemiology, clinical manifestations, diagnosis, and treatment with a focus on environmental survival, susceptibility to antiseptics and surface disinfectants, and infection prevention.
Consensus and conclusions
Mpox, Nipah virus, and Andes virus all survive in the environment and are capable of indirect transmission. All these viruses are enveloped and susceptible to commonly used antiseptics and surface disinfectants.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not report specific controversies or evidence gaps related to the Mpox virus.
Research and practice implications
Rapid identification of patients with known or suspected infections of these emerging viruses coupled with adherence to infection prevention recommendations will prevent healthcare-associated transmission.
