猴痘病毒 的综合分子、流行病学和生物信息学观点:对 监测 和全球健康准备的影响。

Integrated molecular, epidemiological, and bioinformatics perspectives on the Mpox virus: Implications for surveillance and Global Health preparedness.
作者： Rajtilak Detroja, Muktesh Chandra
摘要中文翻译猴痘 重新成为全球重大人畜共患威胁,主要由 2022 年全球分支 IIb 暴发 和 2024 年中部非洲分支 Ib 流行这两大浪潮推动。这篇综述从分子、流行病学和生物信息学的角度审视了解释这种不断演变的病毒所面临的挑战,重点关注全球卫生人力的准备情况。分支IIb主要通过性传播在各国传播,但分支Ib出现在更广泛的人群中——妇女、儿童和通过家庭传播而没有任何性接触而感染的个人。早期病例系列表明,Clade Ib 可能会造成更严重的疾病负担,但由于目前研究数量有限,需要更多的研究来直接比较 Clade IIb 的严重程度和死亡率。该综述研究了该病毒在其约 197 kbp 基因组 中逃避 宿主 免疫防御的策略,包括它如何破坏干扰素信号传导并创建诱饵受体。这篇综述总结了 PALM007 和 STOMP 的临床结果,指出这两项试验都没有达到其主要疗效终点,使得常规 特考韦瑞 的使用不那么引人注目,但它是否有助于特定的高危人群仍悬而未决。更进一步的一点是,对 MVA-BN 疫苗 的免疫力会随着时间的推移而减弱,导致增强剂 疫苗接种 的采用越来越多。总之,该审查呼吁采用一种将基因组跟踪与生态智能相结合的“同一个健康”方法,并将废水 监测 纳入其中,以填补现有的知识空白,并加强全球对新 正痘病毒 威胁的处理。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注 猴痘病毒,检查其分子、流行病学和生物信息学特征,特别是其 传播 模式、免疫逃避策略以及对全球卫生准备的影响。核心问题是这些方面如何影响 监测 以及对 猴痘 暴发 的响应,特别是考虑到 2022 年 Clade IIb 和 2024 Clade Ib 流行。
证据基础与方法
该综述利用分子、流行病学和生物信息学数据对 猴痘病毒 进行了系统分析。它包括病例系列和临床试验,例如 PALM007 和 STOMP,并讨论了基因组追踪、生态智能和废水 监测 的使用作为其方法的一部分。
共识与结论
猴痘病毒,特别是进化枝 Ib 和进化枝 IIb,具有不同的 传播 模式和疾病严重程度概况。进化枝 Ib 似乎传播得更广泛,影响非性人群,而进化枝 IIb 主要通过性传播。该病毒还采用免疫逃避策略,例如破坏干扰素信号传导和创建诱饵受体。
争议与缺口
由于研究数量较少,对 Ib 型分支和 IIb 型分支之间的严重程度和死亡率的直接比较有限。此外,特考韦瑞 在常规使用中的疗效仍不确定,尽管它可能对特定的高危人群有益。该评论还强调需要对 免疫应答 以及助推器 疫苗接种 的作用进行更多研究。
科研与实践启示
该评论强调了整合基因组追踪、生态智能和废水 监测 的“同一个健康”方法对于改善全球准备情况的重要性。它还表明需要进一步研究免疫应答、疫苗的疗效,并针对高危人群制定针对性的干预措施。

Integrated molecular, epidemiological, and bioinformatics perspectives on the Mpox virus: Implications for surveillance and Global Health preparedness.

Authors: Rajtilak Detroja, Muktesh Chandra
Original abstract (English)
Mpox has re-emerged as a significant global zoonotic threat, driven mainly by two large waves the 2022 worldwide Clade IIb outbreak and the 2024 Clade Ib epidemic in Central Africa. This review examines the challenges of interpreting this evolving virus from molecular, epidemiological, and bioinformatics perspectives, with a focus on global health workforce preparedness. Clade IIb largely moved through sexual transmission across countries, but Clade Ib has appeared in a wider population-women, children, and individuals infected through household spread without any sexual contact. Early case series suggest that Clade Ib may cause a more severe disease burden, but more research is needed to directly compare severity and fatality rates with Clade IIb due to the limited number of current studies. The review examines the virus's strategies for evading the host's immune defenses throughout its ~197 kbp genome, including how it disrupts interferon signaling and creates decoy receptors. This review summarizes the clinical findings of PALM007 and STOMP, noting that neither trial achieved its main efficacy endpoint making routine tecovirimat use less compelling-while leaving open whether it helps particular high-risk groups. A further point is that immunity from the MVA-BN vaccine wanes with time, leading to the growing adoption of booster vaccinations. In conclusion, the review calls for a One Health approach pairing genomic tracking with ecological intelligence and including wastewater surveillance to fill existing gaps in knowledge and enhance the global handling of new orthopoxvirus threats.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on the Mpox virus, examining its molecular, epidemiological, and bioinformatics characteristics, particularly its transmission patterns, immune evasion strategies, and implications for global health preparedness. The central question is how these aspects influence surveillance and response to mpox outbreaks, especially in light of the 2022 Clade IIb and 2024 Clade Ib epidemics.
Evidence base and method
The review is a systematic analysis of the Mpox virus, drawing on molecular, epidemiological, and bioinformatics data. It includes case series and clinical trials such as PALM007 and STOMP, and discusses the use of genomic tracking, ecological intelligence, and wastewater surveillance as part of its methodology.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that the Mpox virus, particularly Clade Ib and Clade IIb, has distinct transmission patterns and disease severity profiles. Clade Ib appears to spread more widely, affecting non-sexual populations, while Clade IIb is primarily transmitted sexually. The virus also employs immune evasion strategies, such as disrupting interferon signaling and creating decoy receptors.
Controversies and gaps
There is limited direct comparison of severity and fatality rates between Clade Ib and Clade IIb due to the small number of studies. Additionally, the efficacy of tecovirimat in routine use remains uncertain, though it may be beneficial for specific high-risk groups. The review also highlights the need for more research on immune responses and the role of booster vaccinations.
Research and practice implications
The review emphasizes the importance of a One Health approach that integrates genomic tracking, ecological intelligence, and wastewater surveillance to improve global preparedness. It also suggests the need for further research on immune responses, vaccine efficacy, and the development of targeted interventions for high-risk populations.

欧洲的 猴痘,2022-2026 年:病毒进化枝的范围界定审查、宿主 严重程度的决定因素和 抗病毒药物 治疗

Mpox in Europe, 2022–2026: A Scoping Review of Viral Clades, Host Determinants of Severity and Antiviral Therapy
作者： Filippos Sofos, Zoi Dorothea Pana, Dimitris Drikakis
摘要中文翻译欧洲 2022-2024 年 猴痘 暴发 以 MPXV Clade IIb 为主,总体温和,但最近的 Clade I/Ib 检测和早期当地 传播 改变了准备需求。我们对 2022 年至 2026 年欧洲 猴痘 证据进行了 PRISMA-ScR 范围界定审查,并补充了区域证据稀疏的全球和非洲数据,涵盖分支、严重疾病的 宿主 决定因素以及 抗病毒药物 疗效和耐药性,并对证据差距进行了排名。欧洲分支 IIb 监测 的 死亡率 值非常低(22,662 例病例中有 10 人死亡;病死率为 0.04%),而受影响的非洲地区分支 I/Ib 的估计值为 3-11%,尽管比较因年龄、卫生系统可及性和合并症而混淆。欧洲的严重疾病主要是由免疫损害驱动的:非 HIV 免疫抑制、不受控制的 HIV 和 CD4 计数 < 200 个细胞/μL 是最一致的标志物,而女性仅占少数病例,住院风险较高,尤其是在怀孕期间。特考韦瑞 在四项随机试验中未能改善结果,晚期 HIV 中报告了 F13L 相关耐药性,其欧洲 猴痘 的使用随后受到限制。对于免疫功能正常的成年人中轻度 Clade IIb 感染的证据基础最强,而对于最有可能需要 治疗 的群体来说证据基础最弱。准备工作应优先考虑统一的 基因组监测、严重程度分层的 队列研究 数据以及招募免疫功能低下、儿科、孕妇和老年患者的适应性治疗试验。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注猴痘病毒(MPXV),专门检查2022年至2026年欧洲的病毒进化枝、疾病严重程度的宿主决定因素以及抗病毒药物治疗效果。中心问题是了解流行病学、临床特征和猴痘在该地区的治疗选择,重点是分支特异性差异和 宿主 相关风险因素。
证据基础与方法
使用 2022 年至 2026 年欧洲 猴痘 证据进行了 PRISMA-ScR 范围界定审查,并辅以区域证据稀少的全球和非洲数据。该综述旨在涵盖 MPXV 进化枝、严重疾病的 宿主 决定因素以及 抗病毒药物 疗效和耐药性,同时还确定证据差距。
共识与结论
欧洲 2022-2024 年 猴痘 暴发 由 MPXV Clade IIb 主导,死亡率 非常低(0.04%)。然而,最近检测到的 Clade I/Ib 和早期本地 传播 引起了人们的担忧。欧洲的严重疾病主要与免疫受损有关,包括非 HIV 免疫抑制、不受控制的 HIV 和低 CD4 计数。女性,尤其是孕妇,表现出更高的住院风险。
争议与缺口
欧洲和非洲 猴痘 结果之间的比较受到年龄、卫生系统可及性和合并症等因素的影响。关于 特考韦瑞 对 Clade I/Ib 感染的疗效的证据有限,并且由于报告的耐药性,其在欧洲的使用受到限制。对于免疫功能正常的成年人中轻度 Clade IIb 感染的证据基础最强,而对于最有可能需要 治疗 的群体来说证据基础最弱。
科研与实践启示
准备工作应优先考虑统一的 基因组监测、严重程度分层的 队列研究 数据以及包括免疫功能低下、儿童、孕妇和老年患者的适应性治疗试验。需要进一步研究来了解 Clade I/Ib 感染的临床结果,并为高危人群制定有针对性的干预措施。

Mpox in Europe, 2022–2026: A Scoping Review of Viral Clades, Host Determinants of Severity and Antiviral Therapy

Authors: Filippos Sofos, Zoi Dorothea Pana, Dimitris Drikakis
Original abstract (English)
The 2022–2024 mpox outbreak in Europe was dominated by MPXV Clade IIb and generally mild, but recent Clade I/Ib detections and early local transmission have changed preparedness needs. We performed a PRISMA-ScR scoping review of European mpox evidence from 2022 to 2026, supplemented by global and African data where regional evidence was sparse, covering clades, host determinants of severe disease, and antiviral efficacy and resistance, and ranking the evidence gaps. European Clade IIb surveillance showed very low mortality (10 deaths among 22,662 cases; case fatality 0.04%), contrasting with 3–11% estimates for Clade I/Ib in affected African settings, although comparisons are confounded by age, health-system access and comorbidity. Severe European disease was driven mainly by immune compromise: non-HIV immunosuppression, uncontrolled HIV and CD4 counts < 200 cells/μL were the most consistent markers, while women were a small minority of cases, with higher hospitalisation risk, especially in pregnancy. Tecovirimat failed to improve outcomes in four randomised trials, F13L-associated resistance was reported in advanced HIV, and its European mpox use was subsequently restricted. The evidence base is strongest for mild Clade IIb infection in immunocompetent adults and weakest for the groups most likely to need treatment. Preparedness should prioritise harmonised genomic surveillance, severity-stratified cohort data and adaptive therapeutic trials enrolling immunocompromised, paediatric, pregnant and older patients.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the Mpox virus (MPXV), specifically examining viral clades, host determinants of disease severity, and antiviral therapy efficacy in Europe from 2022 to 2026. The central question is to understand the epidemiology, clinical features, and treatment options of mpox in this region, with an emphasis on clade-specific differences and host-related risk factors.
Evidence base and method
A PRISMA-ScR scoping review was conducted using European mpox evidence from 2022 to 2026, supplemented by global and African data where regional evidence was sparse. The review aimed to cover MPXV clades, host determinants of severe disease, and antiviral efficacy and resistance, while also identifying evidence gaps.
Consensus and conclusions
The 2022–2024 mpox outbreak in Europe was dominated by MPXV Clade IIb, with very low mortality (0.04%). However, recent detections of Clade I/Ib and early local transmission have raised concerns. Severe disease in Europe was primarily associated with immune compromise, including non-HIV immunosuppression, uncontrolled HIV, and low CD4 counts. Women, especially pregnant individuals, showed a higher hospitalisation risk.
Controversies and gaps
Comparisons between European and African mpox outcomes are confounded by factors such as age, health-system access, and comorbidity. There is limited evidence on the efficacy of tecovirimat in Clade I/Ib infections, and its use in Europe was restricted due to reported resistance. The evidence base is strongest for mild Clade IIb infections in immunocompetent adults and weakest for groups most likely to require treatment.
Research and practice implications
Preparedness should prioritize harmonized genomic surveillance, severity-stratified cohort data, and adaptive therapeutic trials that include immunocompromised, pediatric, pregnant, and older patients. Further research is needed to understand the clinical outcomes of Clade I/Ib infections and to develop targeted interventions for high-risk groups.

用于 发病机制 研究的工程类器官平台。

Engineering Organoid Platforms for Pathogenesis Research.
作者： Xiaolu Han, Jun Zhang, Jiahui Bai, Weiming Tian, Jie Zhou, Lihua Ding, Xiang Gao
摘要中文翻译从 1918 年的 H1N1 流感大流行到最近的 SARS-CoV-2 和猴痘病毒 暴发,新出现和重新出现的传染病继续对全球公共卫生构成深刻威胁。这些危机凸显了对高保真度和人类相关感染模型的迫切需求。类器官技术通过忠实地再现体外天然人体组织的 3 维结构和生理微环境,已成为病原体研究的基石平台。这篇综述系统地研究了基于类器官的感染模型的发展和结构完善,重点是干细胞来源选择、细胞外基质优化和动态培养系统工程的循证策略。这些进步使得能够稳健地生成多器官模型,包括呼吸系统、肠道和神经类器官,专门用于研究病毒向性、时空感染动力学和 宿主 免疫应答。此外,我们评估了类器官在高通量 抗病毒药物 药物筛选和临床前 疫苗 评估中的转化效用。为了进一步增强生理相关性和功能保真度,类器官平台越来越多地与先进的工程策略相结合,包括共培养方法、CRISPR-Cas9介导的遗传扰动、工程微生理系统(例如芯片上的器官)和3D生物打印。这些集成技术改进了仿生学,同时扩大了实验的可控性和可扩展性。此外,我们批判性地研究限制临床转化的主要瓶颈,并讨论由人工智能和合成生物学驱动的新兴前沿。通过迭代技术改进和跨学科融合,类器官已经超越还原论的体外替代品,发展成为生理学和机制驱动的平台,促进我们对 宿主 与病原体相互作用的理解,同时增强全球针对新出现病原体的准备。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注用于研究 猴痘病毒 的基于类器官的感染模型的开发和结构完善,重点是了解病毒向性、时空感染动力学和 宿主 免疫应答。核心问题是如何优化类器官平台,使其成为 猴痘病毒 研究的高保真、人类相关模型。
证据基础与方法
该综述系统地研究了基于类器官的感染模型的发展,重点是干细胞来源选择、细胞外基质优化和动态培养系统工程的循证策略。它评估类器官在高通量 抗病毒药物 药物筛选和临床前 疫苗 评估中的转化效用。
共识与结论
类器官技术已成为病原体研究的基石平台,能够生成强大的多器官模型,用于研究病毒向性、时空感染动力学和 宿主 免疫应答。这些模型通过与共培养方法、CRISPR-Cas9 介导的遗传扰动和 3D 生物打印等先进工程策略的集成得到增强。
争议与缺口
该评论批判性地审查了限制类器官平台临床转化的主要瓶颈,并讨论了人工智能和合成生物学驱动的新兴前沿。然而,所提供的证据并未报告与 猴痘病毒 相关的具体争议或证据差距。
科研与实践启示
该评论强调需要进一步改进技术和跨学科融合,以增强类器官平台的生理相关性和功能保真度。它还强调了评估类器官在高通量 抗病毒药物 药物筛选和临床前 疫苗 评估中的转化效用的重要性。

Engineering Organoid Platforms for Pathogenesis Research.

Authors: Xiaolu Han, Jun Zhang, Jiahui Bai, Weiming Tian, Jie Zhou, Lihua Ding, Xiang Gao
Original abstract (English)
Emerging and re-emerging infectious diseases ranging from the 1918 H1N1 influenza pandemic to the recent SARS-CoV-2 and monkeypox virus outbreaks continue to pose profound threats to global public health. These crises underscore the critical need for high-fidelity and human-relevant infection models. Organoid technology has emerged as a cornerstone platform for pathogen research by faithfully recapitulating the 3-dimensional architecture and physiological microenvironment of native human tissues in vitro. This review systematically examines the development and structural refinement of organoid-based infection models with an emphasis on evidence-based strategies for stem cell source selection, extracellular matrix optimization, and dynamic culture system engineering. Such advancements enable the robust generation of multi-organ models including respiratory, intestinal, and neural organoids tailored for investigating viral tropism, spatiotemporal infection kinetics, and host immune responses. Furthermore, we evaluate the translational utility of organoids in high-throughput antiviral drug screening and preclinical vaccine assessment. To further enhance physiological relevance and functional fidelity, organoid platforms are being increasingly combined with advanced engineering strategies, including coculture approaches, CRISPR-Cas9-mediated genetic perturbation, engineered microphysiological systems (such as organ-on-a-chip), and 3D bioprinting. These integrated technologies improve biomimicry while expanding experimental controllability and scalability. In addition, we critically examine the major bottlenecks limiting clinical translation and discuss emerging frontiers driven by artificial intelligence and synthetic biology. Through iterative technological refinement and cross-disciplinary convergence, organoids have evolved beyond reductionist in vitro surrogates into physiologically informed and mechanism-driven platforms that advance our understanding of host-pathogen interactions while enhancing global preparedness against emerging pathogens.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on the development and structural refinement of organoid-based infection models for studying the Mpox virus, with an emphasis on understanding viral tropism, spatiotemporal infection kinetics, and host immune responses. The central question is how organoid platforms can be optimized to serve as high-fidelity, human-relevant models for Mpox virus research.
Evidence base and method
The review systematically examines the development of organoid-based infection models, with an emphasis on evidence-based strategies for stem cell source selection, extracellular matrix optimization, and dynamic culture system engineering. It evaluates the translational utility of organoids in high-throughput antiviral drug screening and preclinical vaccine assessment.
Consensus and conclusions
Organoid technology has emerged as a cornerstone platform for pathogen research, enabling the robust generation of multi-organ models tailored for investigating viral tropism, spatiotemporal infection kinetics, and host immune responses. These models are being enhanced through integration with advanced engineering strategies such as coculture approaches, CRISPR-Cas9-mediated genetic perturbation, and 3D bioprinting.
Controversies and gaps
The review critically examines the major bottlenecks limiting clinical translation of organoid platforms and discusses emerging frontiers driven by artificial intelligence and synthetic biology. However, the supplied evidence does not report specific controversies or evidence gaps related to the Mpox virus.
Research and practice implications
The review highlights the need for further technological refinement and cross-disciplinary convergence to enhance the physiological relevance and functional fidelity of organoid platforms. It also emphasizes the importance of evaluating the translational utility of organoids in high-throughput antiviral drug screening and preclinical vaccine assessment.

用于监测针对病毒感染的结合、阻断和替代中和抗体反应的纸基生物传感器

Paper-Based Biosensors for Monitoring Binding, Blocking, and Surrogate Neutralizing Antibody Responses Against Viral Infections
作者： Yiren Yin, Yujie Yi, Yazheng Yu, Tatyana Aleksandrovna Khrustaleva, Linlin Zhai, Jianhai Yu, Wei Zhao, Chenguang Shen
摘要中文翻译病毒特异性抗体反应,包括结合抗体和中和抗体(nAb),是感染或免疫后 抗病毒药物 免疫状态的重要指标。它们提供了感染后 抗病毒药物 体液免疫或 疫苗接种 的补充信息。抗原结合抗体表明之前的暴露情况和 免疫应答 的大小,而受体阻断和功能中和测定则评估抗体是否干扰病毒进入或感染。传统的中和测定,例如噬菌斑减少中和试验和假病毒中和试验,可以提供功能信息,但劳动密集型、耗时、生物安全性受限,并且难以部署用于大规模或分散监测。纸基生物传感器,包括侧流测定 (LFA)、微流控纸基分析装置 (μPAD) 和纸基 ELISA,由于其成本低、便携、操作简单以及与视觉或数字读数的兼容性,已成为有前途的护理点工具。本综述根据这些平台是否测量抗原结合抗体、受体阻断活性、替代中和或功能中和对这些平台进行了批判性评估,并总结了这三个平台在监测针对 SARS-CoV-2、流感、登革热、寨卡和猴痘病毒的抗体反应方面的应用。与之前主要关注一般纸质生物传感器设计或传统 nAb 测定的综述不同,本综述强调抗原结合、受体阻断和替代中和读数之间的区别,并批判性地讨论了如何解释与功能免疫相关的纸质信号。我们进一步分析关键的转化挑战,包括定量准确性、抗原交叉反应性、标准化、临床验证、监管定位和实际实施。未来的发展应结合多重检测、标准化校准、数字和人工智能辅助解释以及临床验证的检测格式。纸基生物传感器在分散抗体监测和公共卫生 监测 方面具有相当大的潜力,但其临床效用取决于明确的测定定位和针对适当功能或参考方法的验证。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述研究了用于监测针对病毒感染的抗体反应的纸质生物传感器,重点关注其测量抗原结合抗体、受体阻断活性、替代中和或功能中和的能力,特别适用于 猴痘病毒 等病原体。
证据基础与方法
该综述根据纸质生物传感器平台的设计、操作简单性、便携性以及与视觉或数字读数的兼容性,对纸质生物传感器平台进行了评估,包括侧流分析、微流体纸质分析设备和纸质 ELISA。
共识与结论
纸基生物传感器为抗体监测提供了低成本、便携式和简单的即时护理工具,但其临床实用性取决于明确的测定定位和针对功能或参考方法的验证,以确保 免疫应答 的准确解释。
争议与缺口
主要的转化挑战包括定量准确性、抗原交叉反应性、标准化、临床验证、监管定位和现实世界的实施,这些挑战限制了这些平台的广泛采用。
科研与实践启示
未来的发展应优先考虑多重检测、标准化校准、数字和人工智能辅助解释以及临床验证的测定格式,以增强纸质生物传感器的可靠性和可扩展性,用于分散抗体监测和公共卫生监测。

Paper-Based Biosensors for Monitoring Binding, Blocking, and Surrogate Neutralizing Antibody Responses Against Viral Infections

Authors: Yiren Yin, Yujie Yi, Yazheng Yu, Tatyana Aleksandrovna Khrustaleva, Linlin Zhai, Jianhai Yu, Wei Zhao, Chenguang Shen
Original abstract (English)
Virus-specific antibody responses, including binding antibodies and neutralizing antibodies (nAbs), are important indicators of antiviral immune status after infection or immunization. They provide complementary information on antiviral humoral immunity after infection or vaccination. Antigen-binding antibodies indicate previous exposure and the magnitude of the immune response, whereas receptor-blocking and functional neutralization assays assess whether antibodies interfere with viral entry or infection. Conventional neutralization assays, such as plaque reduction neutralization tests and pseudovirus neutralization tests, provide functional information but are labor-intensive, time-consuming, biosafety-restricted, and difficult to deploy for large-scale or decentralized monitoring. Paper-based biosensors, including lateral flow assays (LFAs), microfluidic paper-based analytical devices (μPADs), and paper-based ELISA, have emerged as promising point-of-care tools owing to their low cost, portability, simple operation, and compatibility with visual or digital readouts. This review critically evaluates these platforms according to whether they measure antigen-binding antibodies, receptor-blocking activity, surrogate neutralization, or functional neutralization and summarizes the applications of these three platforms for monitoring antibody responses against SARS-CoV-2, influenza, dengue, Zika, and monkeypox viruses. Unlike previous reviews that mainly focus on general paper-based biosensor design or conventional nAb assays, this review emphasizes the distinction between antigen-binding, receptor-blocking, and surrogate neutralization readouts, and critically discusses how paper-based signals should be interpreted in relation to functional immunity. We further analyze key translational challenges, including quantitative accuracy, antigen cross-reactivity, standardization, clinical validation, regulatory positioning, and real-world implementation. Future development should combine multiplex detection, standardized calibration, digital and AI-assisted interpretation, and clinically validated assay formats. Paper-based biosensors have considerable potential for decentralized antibody monitoring and public health surveillance, but their clinical utility depends on clear assay positioning and validation against appropriate functional or reference methods.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines paper-based biosensors for monitoring antibody responses against viral infections, focusing on their ability to measure antigen-binding antibodies, receptor-blocking activity, surrogate neutralization, or functional neutralization, with specific applications to mpox virus among other pathogens.
Evidence base and method
The review evaluates paper-based biosensor platforms, including lateral flow assays, microfluidic paper-based analytical devices, and paper-based ELISA, based on their design, operational simplicity, portability, and compatibility with visual or digital readouts.
Consensus and conclusions
Paper-based biosensors offer low-cost, portable, and simple point-of-care tools for antibody monitoring, but their clinical utility depends on clear assay positioning and validation against functional or reference methods to ensure accurate interpretation of immune responses.
Controversies and gaps
Key translational challenges include quantitative accuracy, antigen cross-reactivity, standardization, clinical validation, regulatory positioning, and real-world implementation, which limit the widespread adoption of these platforms.
Research and practice implications
Future development should prioritize multiplex detection, standardized calibration, digital and AI-assisted interpretation, and clinically validated assay formats to enhance the reliability and scalability of paper-based biosensors for decentralized antibody monitoring and public health surveillance.

当前选择重组 正痘病毒 蛋白用于免疫生物学和诊断应用的策略

Current Strategies for Selecting Recombinant Orthopoxvirus Proteins for Immunobiological and Diagnostic Applications
作者： Aigerim Zhakypbek, Lespek KUTUMBETOV, Гулнур Кузембекова, Kamshat Shorayeva, Nurlan Kozhabergenov, Bekbolat Usserbayev, Gulnur Nakhanova, Kuanysh Jekebekov, G. O. Shynybekova, Akbope A. Abdykalyk
摘要中文翻译正如最近的全球 猴痘 暴发 所强调的那样,正痘病毒 是人畜共患 DNA 病毒,对公共卫生具有持续重要性。正痘病毒 之间的高抗原相似性使得 免疫应答 能够发生交叉反应,但使用于血清监测、评估既往暴露、评估 疫苗 诱导的免疫和物种分化的血清学诊断系统的开发变得复杂。本综述总结了有关主要 正痘病毒 重组抗原的结构、生物学功能以及诊断和 疫苗 潜力的当前知识。特别关注 正痘病毒 的基因组组织、细胞内成熟病毒颗粒 (IMV) 和细胞外有包膜病毒颗粒 (EV) 形式之间的差异,以及关键抗原的特征,包括 A29、M1、H3、A35 和 B6。比较系统发育分析表明,结构重要的 正痘病毒 蛋白之间具有高度保守性,同时还鉴定了可能与鉴别诊断相关的可变区域。现有证据表明,IMV 相关蛋白主要参与病毒中和,而 EV 相关抗原主要有助于限制病毒传播。因此,结合 IMV 和 EV 相关蛋白的多组分抗原策略似乎为开发有效的血清学检测和下一代亚基 疫苗 提供了最大的潜力。总体而言,合理的抗原选择应基于结构、功能和免疫学特性的综合评估,以优化正痘病毒诊断和疫苗平台。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注用于免疫生物学和诊断应用的重组 正痘病毒 蛋白的选择,特别是针对 猴痘病毒 及其进化枝。核心问题是如何在开发有效的诊断工具和 疫苗 时优化这些蛋白质的使用。
证据基础与方法
该综述基于比较系统发育分析,并整合了关键 正痘病毒 蛋白的结构、功能和免疫学特性。它没有明确报告数据库、搜索日期或荟萃分析方法。
共识与结论
该综述得出的结论是,结合 IMV 和 EV 相关蛋白的多组分抗原策略为有效的血清学检测和亚基 疫苗 提供了最大的潜力。它强调了整合结构、功能和免疫学特性对于合理抗原选择的重要性。
争议与缺口
该评论指出,虽然结构上重要的 正痘病毒 蛋白之间存在高度保守性,但存在可能与鉴别诊断相关的可变区域。然而,证据并未提供有关这些可变区域的具体机制或影响的详细信息。
科研与实践启示
该综述建议未来的研究应侧重于多组分​​抗原策略的开发以及结构、功能和免疫学数据的整合,以优化 猴痘病毒 的诊断和 疫苗 平台。

Current Strategies for Selecting Recombinant Orthopoxvirus Proteins for Immunobiological and Diagnostic Applications

Authors: Aigerim Zhakypbek, Lespek KUTUMBETOV, Гулнур Кузембекова, Kamshat Shorayeva, Nurlan Kozhabergenov, Bekbolat Usserbayev, Gulnur Nakhanova, Kuanysh Jekebekov, G. O. Shynybekova, Akbope A. Abdykalyk
Original abstract (English)
Orthopoxviruses are zoonotic DNA viruses of continuing public health importance, as highlighted by recent global mpox outbreaks. The high antigenic similarity among orthopoxviruses enables cross-reactive immune responses but complicates the development of serological diagnostic systems for serosurveillance, assessment of previous exposure, evaluation of vaccine-induced immunity, and species differentiation. This review summarizes current knowledge regarding the structure, biological functions, and diagnostic and vaccine potential of major orthopoxvirus recombinant antigens. Particular attention is given to the genomic organization of Orthopoxvirus, differences between intracellular mature virion (IMV) and extracellular enveloped virion (EV) forms, and the characteristics of key antigens, including A29, M1, H3, A35, and B6. Comparative phylogenetic analyses demonstrated a high degree of conservation among structurally important orthopoxvirus proteins while also identifying variable regions potentially relevant for differential diagnostics. Available evidence indicates that IMV-associated proteins are primarily involved in virus neutralization, whereas EV-associated antigens contribute mainly to limiting viral dissemination. Consequently, multicomponent antigen strategies combining IMV- and EV-associated proteins appear to provide the greatest potential for the development of effective serological assays and next-generation subunit vaccines. Overall, rational antigen selection should be based on integrated evaluation of structural, functional, and immunological properties to optimize orthopoxvirus diagnostic and vaccine platforms.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on the selection of recombinant orthopoxvirus proteins for immunobiological and diagnostic applications, specifically addressing the Mpox virus and its clades. The central question is how to optimize the use of these proteins in developing effective diagnostic tools and vaccines.
Evidence base and method
The review is based on comparative phylogenetic analyses and integrates structural, functional, and immunological properties of key orthopoxvirus proteins. It does not explicitly report on databases, search dates, or meta-analysis methods.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that multicomponent antigen strategies combining IMV- and EV-associated proteins offer the greatest potential for effective serological assays and subunit vaccines. It highlights the importance of integrating structural, functional, and immunological properties for rational antigen selection.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that while there is a high degree of conservation among structurally important orthopoxvirus proteins, there are variable regions that may be relevant for differential diagnostics. However, the evidence does not provide detailed information on the specific mechanisms or implications of these variable regions.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that future research should focus on the development of multicomponent antigen strategies and the integration of structural, functional, and immunological data to optimize diagnostic and vaccine platforms for Mpox virus.

A 高优先级公共卫生新闻

猴痘 暴发 在乌干达疫情最新情况报告 - 2024年11月01日 - 世界卫生组织 | 非洲区域办事处

Mpox Outbreak in Uganda Situation Update - 01 November 2024 - WHO | Regional Office for Africa
新闻简报The WHO Regional Office for Africa issued a situation update on 05 August 2026 regarding a mpox outbreak in Uganda. The update notes that thirty seven new confirmed cases were reported in the past 24 hours, which aligns with the cumulative total of 324 confirmed cases and one death. The outbreak has been documented in multiple districts, including Wakiso, Kampala, Mukono, Yumbe, Mayuge, Jinja, Nakasongola, Mubende, Mbarara, Luwero, and Kikuube. The report highlights the ongoing monitoring of the outbreak but does not specify detailed response measures. The situation update is part of a broader effort to track and manage mpox cases in the region, with no mention of a specific risk level. The evidence is sourced from the WHO's official publication, which provides a comprehensive overview of the outbreak's progression and impact.
查看新闻五要素
时间
The situation update was published on 05 August 2026. The evidence mentions that thirty seven new confirmed cases were reported in the past 24 hours, which would be around 01 November 2024.
地点与对象
暴发 发生在乌干达,特别是在瓦基索、坎帕拉、穆科诺、永贝、马尤格、金贾、纳卡松戈拉、穆本德、姆巴拉拉、卢韦罗和基库贝等地区。受影响人群包括已确认患有 猴痘 的个人。
事件
世卫组织非洲区域办事处报告乌干达确诊一例 猴痘 暴发,过去 24 小时报告新增 37 例病例。累计确诊324例,死亡1例。
规模、影响与风险
累计确诊病例324例,死亡1例。暴发已蔓延至乌干达多个地区,对当地居民造成重大影响。风险并未明确说明,但多例病例的存在表明存在潜在的公共卫生问题。
应对、状态与不确定性
世界卫生组织已提供最新情况,表明 暴发 正在接受监测。该报告没有提及具体的应对措施,但它是正在进行的 监测 和报告流程的一部分。

Bavarian Nordic 获得欧盟批准将 猴痘/Smalpox 疫苗 扩大到 2 岁儿童 - BioPharm International

Bavarian Nordic Wins EU Approval to Expand Mpox/Smallpox Vaccine to Children as Young as 2 - BioPharm International
新闻简报Bavarian Nordic 已获得欧盟批准,将其 猴痘/天花 疫苗 扩大到 2 岁及以上儿童,这标志着公共卫生准备工作取得了重大进展。这一决定是在该公司努力扩大 疫苗 的应用范围之后做出的,这可以增强对年轻人群体的 猴痘 暴发 的保护。此次批准是基于 疫苗 现有的功效和安全性概况,但可能需要进一步研究来确认其是否适合儿童。该事件已得到欧盟监管行动的证实,目前正在调查响应状态,以评估此次扩张的更广泛影响。证据中没有报告具体的病例数、死亡数或暴露数量,但此举预计将增加 疫苗 对公共卫生的影响。此次批准反映了解决 猴痘 风险的积极主动方法,特别是在病毒已显示出 猴痘 潜力增加的社区。

Analytics for Bispecific Antibody Product Release Planning the analytical strategy for product release should be started as early as possible to ensure product quality and regulatory success.
地点与对象
欧盟(EU)。
事件
Bavarian Nordic 已获得欧盟批准,将其 猴痘/天花 疫苗 的使用范围扩大到 2 岁儿童。
规模、影响与风险
将 疫苗 扩大到儿童可能会增加其公共卫生影响,但证据中没有报告具体的病例数、死亡人数或暴露人数。
应对、状态与不确定性
该事件已获得欧盟批准,且响应状态正在调查对 疫苗 分发和儿童使用的潜在影响。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Bavarian Nordic Wins EU Approval to Expand Mpox/Smallpox Vaccine to Children as Young as 2 - BioPharm International

News Brief
Bavarian Nordic has secured EU approval to expand its mpox/smallpox vaccine to children aged 2 years and older, marking a significant development in public health preparedness. This decision follows the company's efforts to broaden the vaccine's application, which could enhance protection against mpox outbreaks in younger populations. The approval is based on the vaccine's existing efficacy and safety profiles, though further studies may be required to confirm its suitability for children. The event is confirmed by the EU's regulatory action, and the response status is under investigation to assess the broader implications of this expansion. No specific case counts, death counts, or exposure numbers are reported in the evidence, but the move is expected to increase the vaccine's public health impact. The approval reflects a proactive approach to addressing mpox risks, particularly in communities where the virus has shown increased transmission potential.
Original source text (English)

Location and population
European Union (EU).
Event
Bavarian Nordic has received EU approval to expand the use of its mpox/smallpox vaccine to children as young as 2 years old.
Scale, impact and risk
The expansion of the vaccine to children may increase its public health impact, but no specific case counts, death counts, or exposure numbers are reported in the evidence.
Response, status and uncertainty
The event is confirmed by the EU approval, and the response status is under investigation for potential implications on vaccine distribution and use in children.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

下一代 猴痘 疫苗 显示了持久保护和未来 暴发 准备的承诺 - 当今的感染控制

Next-Generation Mpox Vaccine Shows Promise for Durable Protection and Future Outbreak Preparedness - Infection Control Today
新闻简报Infection Control Today 于 2026 年 8 月 6 日发表的文章重点介绍了下一代 猴痘 疫苗,它显示了持久保护和未来 暴发 准备的承诺。2026 年 7 月 30 日的一篇专题文章讨论了 疫苗 的潜力,强调了其对抗病毒的有效性。2026 年 7 月 29 日的另一篇文章也指出了这一发展的影响,该文章讨论了感染预防策略的重要性。然而,该文章没有提供与 猴痘病毒 相关的具体病例数、死亡数或暴露数量。它还没有详细说明当前的调查状态、测试或隔离措施。重点是 疫苗 对全球卫生准备的潜在影响,而不是直接的 暴发 或其对特定人群或地点的影响。
查看新闻五要素
时间
文章发表于2026-08-06。所讨论的事件,即下一代 猴痘 疫苗 显示出希望,于 19 天前(即 2026 年 7 月 30 日)进行了报道,并于 2026 年 7 月 29 日讨论了研究结果的含义。
地点与对象
本文重点关注全球感染预防实践,并未具体说明受 猴痘病毒 影响的特定地点或人群。
事件
下一代 猴痘 疫苗 被强调,显示出持久保护和未来 暴发 准备的承诺。《今日感染控制》报道了这一事件,并根据提供的证据证实了这一事件。
规模、影响与风险
该文章讨论了 疫苗 对于未来 暴发 准备工作的潜力,但没有报告与 猴痘病毒 相关的具体病例数、死亡人数或暴露数量。证据中没有明确说明风险级别。
应对、状态与不确定性
文章提到了下一代 猴痘 疫苗 的开发及其持久保护和未来 暴发 准备的潜力。不过,它没有提供与 猴痘病毒 相关的当前调查状态、测试或隔离措施的详细信息。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Next-Generation Mpox Vaccine Shows Promise for Durable Protection and Future Outbreak Preparedness - Infection Control Today

News Brief
The article published on August 6, 2026, by Infection Control Today highlights a next-generation Mpox vaccine that shows promise for durable protection and future outbreak preparedness. The vaccine's potential was discussed in a feature article from July 30, 2026, which emphasized its effectiveness in combating the virus. The implications of this development were also noted in another article from July 29, 2026, which discussed the importance of infection prevention strategies. However, the article does not provide specific case counts, death counts, or exposure numbers related to the Mpox virus. It also does not detail the current investigation status, testing, or isolation measures. The focus is on the vaccine's potential impact on global health preparedness rather than on the immediate outbreak or its effects on a specific population or location.

UKHSA 概述:截至 2026 年 7 月 31 日,英国有 119 例 猴痘 分支 Ib 病例,大部分英格兰病例在伦敦 - londondaily.news

UKHSA overview: 119 mpox clade Ib cases in UK to 31 July 2026, majority of England cases in London - londondaily.news
新闻简报英国卫生安全局 (UKHSA) 报告,截至 2026 年 7 月 31 日,英国有 119 例 猴痘 Ib 分支确诊病例,其中大多数病例发生在伦敦。报告还指出,欧洲和北美有报道称,通过同性恋、双性恋和其他男男性行为者 (GBMSM) 网络形成的社区 传播。此外,它还强调了 2025 年 12 月在英国鉴定出的重组 猴痘病毒,其中包含进化枝 Ib 和进化枝 IIb 的元件。截至 2026 年 3 月 31 日,英格兰的 疫苗接种 数据显示,已接种了 101,210 剂第一剂疫苗和 63,915 剂第二剂疫苗,其中伦敦接种第一剂和第二剂疫苗的比例最大。该报告没有对病例的地理分布或 疫苗 递送的集中度提供因果解释,并将这些发现作为流行病学总结的一部分提供,而没有进一步分析。UKHSA 概述是对英国情况的全面总结,包括病例数、区域细分和基因组发现。
查看新闻五要素
时间
The UKHSA overview was published on 08 August 2026, and the case data covers up to 31 July 2026. The report also references findings from December 2025.
地点与对象
UKHSA 报告涵盖英国,重点关注英格兰,特别是伦敦。它还包括北爱尔兰、苏格兰和威尔士。受影响的人群包括欧洲和北美 GBMSM 网络中的个人。
事件
UKHSA 报告确认,截至 2026 年 7 月 31 日,英国已有 119 例 猴痘 Ib 分支病例,其中大多数病例发生在伦敦。它还注意到欧洲和北美通过 GBMSM 网络发现的社区 传播 以及 2025 年 12 月在英国发现的重组 猴痘病毒。
规模、影响与风险
截至2026年7月31日,英国共有119例猴痘 Ib分支病例,其中英格兰82例,北爱尔兰24例,苏格兰7例,威尔士6例。伦敦占案件的大多数,并获得疫苗接种的最大份额。该报告还提到2023年至2026年7月31日期间有1,035起猴痘分支IIb病例,但这些与分支Ib病例没有直接关系。
应对、状态与不确定性
UKHSA 报告提供了截至 2026 年 3 月 31 日英格兰的确诊病例数、区域细分、疫苗接种 总数以及自 2025 年 12 月以来的基因组发现。它没有提供地理分布或 疫苗 递送浓度的因果解释,并且该报告作为流行病学摘要提供,没有进一步解释。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

UKHSA overview: 119 mpox clade Ib cases in UK to 31 July 2026, majority of England cases in London - londondaily.news

News Brief
The UK Health Security Agency (UKHSA) reported 119 confirmed cases of mpox clade Ib in the UK as of 31 July 2026, with the majority of these cases in London. The report also noted that community transmission through gay, bisexual, and other men who have sex with men (GBMSM) networks was reported in Europe and North America. Additionally, it highlighted that a recombinant mpox virus was identified in England in December 2025, which contained elements of both clade Ib and clade IIb. Vaccination data for England up to 31 March 2026 showed that 101,210 first doses and 63,915 second doses were administered, with London receiving the largest share of both first and second doses. The report does not provide causal explanations for the geographic distribution of cases or the concentration of vaccine delivery, and it presents these findings as part of an epidemiological summary without further analysis. The UKHSA overview is a comprehensive summary of the situation in the UK, including case counts, regional breakdowns, and genomic findings.
Original source text (English)
26°C broken clouds TFL Updates Search for: Search Search LDN Write For Us Login London News Lifestyle Entrepreneurship Fitness & Health Finance Tech Food & Drink Automotive Travel Global UKHSA overview: 119 mpox clade Ib cases in UK to 31 July 2026, majority of England cases in London By LDN Editor 08 August 2026 3 minute read Listen to this article UKHSA overview: 119 mpox clade Ib cases in UK to 31 July 2026, majority of England cases in London The UK Health Security Agency (UKHSA) epidemiological overview for 6 August 2026 reports 119 cases of mpox clade Ib in the UK recorded up to 31 July 2026, with most of the England cases reported in London. The same report sets out recent genomic findings, transmission notes and vaccination figures for England to the end of March 2026. Cases at a glance Total mpox clade Ib cases in the UK to 31 July 2026: 119; Distribution of clade Ib cases: 82 in England (the majority in London), 24 in Northern Ireland, 7 in Scotland and 6 in Wales; Separately, between 2023 and 31 July 2026 a total of 1,035 mpox clade IIb cases were reported in the UK: 968 in England, 9 in Northern Ireland, 37 in Scotland and 21 in Wales. Vaccination data for England (to 31 March 2026) The UKHSA overview provides vaccination figures for England up to 31 March 2026. In that period 101,210 first doses and 63,915 second doses of mpox vaccine were administered across England. London accounted for the largest number of first doses, with 63,624 first doses delivered in the capital; the London total for second doses recorded in the report is 42,902. Recent virus and transmission findings The report records that in December 2025 genomic surveillance identified a recombinant mpox virus in England in an individual who had recently travelled to Asia. Sequencing showed the mpox genome in that case contained elements of both clade Ib and clade IIb. The overview also notes that from October 2025 community transmission through gay, bisexual, and other men who have sex with men (GBMSM) networks was reported in Europe and North America. The UKHSA report presents these items as part of its epidemiological summary. Vaccination policy and local offers The report states that the reactive vaccination offer ended nationally in summer 2023. It also records that the reactive offer continued to be provided in London and in Greater Manchester in response to ongoing local transmission and identification of mpox case clusters. What the report does and does not say The UKHSA overview sets out confirmed case counts, the geographic breakdowns above, vaccination totals for England to 31 March 2026 and the sequencing finding from December 2025. The report indicates that most of the 82 clade Ib cases recorded in England were reported in London and that London received the largest share of first doses administered in England. The document does not use the term “epicentre” to describe London, and it does not provide an explicit explanation of the causes of the geographic distribution of cases or of why vaccine delivery was concentrated in particular areas. Causal interpretations of the figures are not provided in the overview and are not confirmed by the report. Practical implications in the report The overview combines case counts, regional breakdowns, vaccination uptake figures and genomic findings in a single epidemiological summary. Readers are advised that the figures cited cover different cut-off dates in the report: cases are recorded to 31 July 2026 for the clade Ib and IIb counts, while the vaccination totals are reported to 31 March 2026. Where the report gives precise numbers or dates these have been used above. Where the report does not provide explanatory or causal statements about the geographic distribution or programme delivery, those elements have not been inferred. The full details are available in the published information. For related coverage, read England and Wales face historic drought following driest July on record. B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

欧盟授权 猴痘 疫苗 用于幼儿,缩小刚果儿童的致命差距 - Tech Times

EU Authorizes Mpox Vaccine for Toddlers, Closing Deadly Gap for DRC Children - Tech Times
新闻简报The European Commission has authorized Bavarian Nordic's IMVANEX vaccine for children aged 2 to under 12 years, marking the first time a non-replicating mpox vaccine has been formally cleared for this age group. This decision, made on August 7, 2026, followed a positive recommendation from the European Medicines Agency (EMA) on June 25, 2026, and was based on a Phase 2 clinical trial (NCT06549530) conducted in the Democratic Republic of Congo (DRC) and Uganda. The trial enrolled 227 children and 224 adults, showing that children's immune responses were 2.5 times greater than adults', with the highest responses in those aged 2 to 5. The vaccine, derived from the vaccinia virus, cannot replicate inside human cells, making it safe for immunocompromised children. However, its peak neutralizing antibody titers are lower than replicating vaccines, and these titers wane within six months. Despite this, the authorization aims to close a critical gap in protecting DRC children, who account for 60 percent of confirmed mpox cases and 80 percent of recorded deaths. The EMA's approval was based on data from the trial, which was submitted on March 27, 2026, and the vaccine's safety profile was found to be comparable to that in adults. The authorization does not resolve broader challenges, such as the need for ongoing monitoring of antibody waning and the establishment of a full immunological correlate of protection.
查看新闻五要素
时间
在 EMA 人用医药产品委员会于 2026 年 6 月 25 日提出积极建议后,欧盟委员会于 2026 年 8 月 7 日授予了上市许可。试验数据于 2026 年 3 月 27 日提交给 EMA,并于 2025 年 10 月公布了主要结果。
地点与对象
The trial was conducted in the Democratic Republic of Congo (DRC) and Uganda, focusing on children aged 2 to under 12 years and adults aged 18 to 50. Children under 15 represent roughly 60 percent of confirmed mpox cases in the DRC and approximately 80 percent of recorded deaths.
事件
欧盟委员会授权 Bavarian Nordic 的 IMVANEX 猴痘 和天花 疫苗 用于 2 岁至 12 岁以下儿童,这标志着非复制 猴痘 疫苗 首次正式获准用于该年龄段。该决定基于在刚果民主共和国和乌干达进行的 2 期临床研究 (NCT06549530)。
规模、影响与风险
Children under 15 account for 60 percent of confirmed mpox cases and 80 percent of recorded deaths in the DRC. The vaccine's authorization aims to address the risk of severe disease and mortality in this vulnerable group, as prior authorization was limited to adults and adolescents.
应对、状态与不确定性
疫苗 现已在欧盟获得授权,适用于所有两岁及以上的人。儿童的安全性与成人相当,没有意外的不良事件。然而,抗体减弱的临床相关性仍不确定,并且尚未确定针对 猴痘 的保护的完整免疫学相关性。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

EU Authorizes Mpox Vaccine for Toddlers, Closing Deadly Gap for DRC Children - Tech Times

News Brief
The European Commission has authorized Bavarian Nordic's IMVANEX vaccine for children aged 2 to under 12 years, marking the first time a non-replicating mpox vaccine has been formally cleared for this age group. This decision, made on August 7, 2026, followed a positive recommendation from the European Medicines Agency (EMA) on June 25, 2026, and was based on a Phase 2 clinical trial (NCT06549530) conducted in the Democratic Republic of Congo (DRC) and Uganda. The trial enrolled 227 children and 224 adults, showing that children's immune responses were 2.5 times greater than adults', with the highest responses in those aged 2 to 5. The vaccine, derived from the vaccinia virus, cannot replicate inside human cells, making it safe for immunocompromised children. However, its peak neutralizing antibody titers are lower than replicating vaccines, and these titers wane within six months. Despite this, the authorization aims to close a critical gap in protecting DRC children, who account for 60 percent of confirmed mpox cases and 80 percent of recorded deaths. The EMA's approval was based on data from the trial, which was submitted on March 27, 2026, and the vaccine's safety profile was found to be comparable to that in adults. The authorization does not resolve broader challenges, such as the need for ongoing monitoring of antibody waning and the establishment of a full immunological correlate of protection.
Original source text (English)
ALTAMONTE SPRINGS, FLORIDA - DECEMBER 16: Vials of smallpox vaccine sit on a counter before a vaccination December 16, 2002 at Mid-Florida Biologicals in Altamonte Springs, Florida. The health center is part of a 16-clinic trial study looking at smallpox vaccinations. According to Dr. Ronald Brown of Mid-Florida Biologicals,, there have been no reports of adverse side effects due to the vaccine among the 2,500 local participants in the study . Chris Livingston/Getty Images The European Commission granted marketing authorization for Bavarian Nordic's IMVANEX mpox and smallpox vaccine to cover children aged 2 to under 12 years — the first time any major regulatory authority has formally cleared a non-replicating mpox vaccine for this age group — closing a gap that left toddlers and school-age children in Africa's worst-hit countries without a formally authorized shot as they bore more than half of the continent's mpox deaths. Children under 15 represent roughly 60 percent of confirmed mpox cases in the Democratic Republic of Congo and approximately 80 percent of recorded deaths — making them the primary victims of an outbreak that has never been primarily a disease of adults in the DRC. This was confirmed by UNICEF data from 2024 . The decision, formally adopted by the Commission on August 7, 2026, converts a positive recommendation issued by the European Medicines Agency's Committee for Medicinal Products for Human Use on June 25, 2026 into a legally binding EU-wide marketing authorization covering all member states and the European Economic Area. The vaccine — known internationally as JYNNEOS in the United States, IMVAMUNE in Canada, and IMVANEX in the EU, the United Kingdom, and the EEA — is now authorized in the EU for everyone aged two and older, as confirmed in Bavarian Nordic's official announcement . Why Children Could Not Get This Vaccine Before The EU gap was structural. IMVANEX received its original EU marketing authorization in 2013 for adults and was extended to adolescents in 2024 to cover ages 12 to 17. That sequential age expansion followed an NIH-sponsored study in adolescents; the 2-to-11 age group required a separate trial, in part because regulators cannot ethically extrapolate pediatric data from adult studies and in part because the populations most affected — DRC children, many of them malnourished or HIV-exposed — live in countries far removed from the trial sites where prior adult and adolescent data were collected. The regulatory gap had real consequences. When 265,000 doses of MVA-BN arrived in the DRC in September 2024 and vaccination began in October of that year, health authorities directed those shots primarily to adults. The children accounting for 80 percent of deaths were not the primary targets of that campaign — because the vaccine had no authorized indication for children, and national regulatory agencies and multilateral bodies follow stringent-regulator clearances when making formal deployment decisions. This is confirmed by UNICEF reporting on DRC vaccination . What the DRC and Uganda Trial Showed The regulatory bridge was a Phase 2 clinical study, NCT06549530, sponsored by Bavarian Nordic and co-funded by the Coalition for Epidemic Preparedness Innovations with a $6.5 million award . The trial enrolled 227 children aged 2 to under 12 years and 224 adults aged 18 to 50 at sites in the DRC and Uganda — both countries experiencing active mpox transmission — which means the immunogenicity data was generated in the precise populations that stand to benefit from the authorization. Each participant received two standard-dose injections four weeks apart. Topline results, announced in October 2025, showed that the immune response in children two weeks after the second dose was non-inferior to that in adults — meaning the vaccine produced at least as strong a response in children as it did in the comparator group. Across the full pediatric cohort, immune responses were 2.5 times greater than in the adult group, with the highest responses observed in the youngest subgroup of children aged 2 to 5. Bavarian Nordic submitted the data to the EMA on March 27, 2026, as confirmed in the EMA submission announcement , and the CHMP adopted its positive opinion at its June 22–25, 2026 meeting. The safety profile in children was comparable to that in adults, with no unexpected adverse events. The World Health Organization's Global Advisory Committee on Vaccine Safety, which reviewed MVA-BN safety data including in special populations through its May 2025 report, found no safety concerns identified in children, HIV-positive individuals, or other vulnerable groups assessed, though it noted that some subgroup sample sizes were small. Why Non-Replication Is the Critical Technical Detail Most discussion of IMVANEX treats it simply as "the mpox vaccine." The technical distinction that makes it irreplaceable for DRC children is not often explained: MVA-BN cannot replicate inside human cells. To understand why that matters, consider how the vaccine was made. MVA (Modified Vaccinia Ankara) was derived from vaccinia virus — the same viral family used in traditional smallpox vaccination — by serial passage more than 500 times through chicken embryo fibroblast cells in Munich between 1953 and 1968. That process caused the virus to accumulate approximately six major deletions in its genome, together accounting for roughly 10 percent of its ancestral genetic information. Among the genes lost were those that allow vaccinia to complete its replication cycle in most mammalian cells. Today, MVA-BN can enter human cells and express its genes — triggering the immune response that provides protection against mpox and smallpox — but the replication cycle is blocked at the virion assembly stage: immature particles form inside cells but cannot be released to spread infection. That single biological constraint has major clinical significance for the children at the center of the DRC outbreak. First-generation replicating smallpox vaccines such as ACAM2000 and Dryvax are contraindicated in immunocompromised individuals because a live-replicating vaccinia virus can cause serious disease in people whose immune systems cannot control it. Malnutrition-related immunosuppression and HIV — both common in the DRC and among mpox-affected children in the region — fall squarely into that contraindication. MVA-BN does not carry this risk, because replication cannot proceed. It is the only authorized mpox vaccine that can be safely administered to immunocompromised children. There is a tradeoff. Because MVA-BN does not replicate, the peak neutralizing antibody titers it induces are roughly 10 to 20 times lower than those produced by replicating vaccines, and those titers wane within six months toward baseline after the two-dose course. Memory B cells remain detectable at one year, and T-cell responses are more durable, but the full immunological correlate of protection against mpox has not been established. The clinical relevance of the antibody waning question remains uncertain. What the NCT06549530 data do confirm is that the immune response across the full pediatric cohort substantially exceeded the adult response — suggesting that children may mount a more robust reaction than the two-dose schedule was designed around in adults. From Brussels to Kinshasa: What the Authorization Does Not Solve EU regulatory clearance carries real weight beyond European borders. EMA assessments serve as the template for WHO prequalification, which in turn enables United Nations agencies and multilateral procurement bodies to purchase and distribute the vaccine in countries lacking the regulatory infrastructure for independent review. The original WHO prequalification of MVA-BN — granted September 13, 2024, for adults — was built on EMA's prior adult authorization, and the DRC's own emergency use approval referenced the EMA and FDA decisions. The 2026 pediatric extension will follow the same pattern: a fresh WHO prequalification covering children aged 2 to under 12 is expected, which will give international procurement bodies formal authority to direct doses specifically to children. But formal authorization is not the same as delivered doses. As of May 2025, a WHO committee reviewing vaccination progress noted that over 651,000 doses of MVA-BN had been administered on the African continent, with 96 percent of those doses going to the DRC. That figure reflects the scale of the challenge: a continent with over a billion people saw fewer than 700,000 total doses reach outbreak-affected populations, and even that limited supply faced significant cold chain and logistics hurdles. The DRC's vast geography and weak infrastructure in conflict-affected provinces mean that reaching children — who may live in internally displaced persons camps in North Kivu or in communities with no functional cold chain equipment — requires a different kind of delivery architecture than an EU-style vaccination campaign. Independent research has documented the specific bottlenecks in mpox vaccine access : cold chain limitations, shortages of trained healthcare workers, inadequate last-mile distribution networks, and underresourced national procurement mechanisms. These barriers do not disappear when a regulatory document is signed in Brussels. Bavarian Nordic and CEPI, whose partnership has also launched separate trials covering infants under two and pregnant women, have acknowledged the gap between authorization and access. CEPI's Mpox Programme Lead Kristine Rose has publicly emphasized that vaccines can only protect lives if they reach those who need them, and that children have too often been among the last to benefit from vaccine development. The EC approval announced August 7 addresses the regulatory barrier; the operational barriers remain . IMVANEX After August 7 With this authorization, MVA-BN now carries formal EU approval across the full post-infancy age range — from two years through adulthood — for active immunization against mpox, smallpox, and vaccinia virus disease. The EU authorization runs across 27 member states plus the broader EEA, and the UK authorization mirrors EU decisions for IMVANEX independently. The EMA originally cleared IMVANEX in 2013 under "exceptional circumstances" — a regulatory pathway used when comprehensive efficacy data cannot be generated under normal conditions, which in this case reflects the eradication of smallpox and the difficulty of conducting controlled mpox efficacy trials in endemic settings. The exceptional-circumstances designation means the product remains under additional monitoring even after each age-group extension, with ongoing safety data requirements. The August 7 decision extends that framework to children aged 2 to under 12. Bavarian Nordic is simultaneously conducting a separate clinical trial program for infants under two years of age and for pregnant and breastfeeding women, launched June 2025 in DRC with CEPI co-funding. Those trials are expected to produce data that could support a further label expansion for the youngest children and for pregnant populations — the remaining vulnerable groups without formal EU authorization. The path from trial initiation to regulatory decision for those populations is likely to take several more years. Frequently Asked Questions Can children in the United States now get the IMVANEX mpox vaccine? No — this is a European Union authorization only. In the United States, the equivalent vaccine (sold as JYNNEOS) is approved by the FDA for adults 18 and older. The FDA granted an emergency use authorization for adolescents during the 2022 mpox outbreak but has not extended a full authorization to children under 18. The EU decision creates pressure and a scientific evidence base that could support an FDA pediatric submission, but US clearance would require a separate regulatory process. Parents of young children in the US who are concerned about mpox exposure should discuss options with their pediatrician. Why are young children in Africa so much more at risk from mpox than children in Europe or the United States? Several compounding factors increase pediatric severity and mortality in the DRC specifically. The current dominant strain — mpox Clade Ib — is associated with higher virulence and easier human-to-human transmission than the Clade IIb strain that drove the 2022 global outbreak in adults. Children in outbreak-affected DRC regions often have malnutrition-related immunosuppression or HIV exposure, both of which impair the immune response to the virus. Cross-protective immunity from the smallpox vaccination campaign that ended in 1980 is absent in everyone born after that period, leaving all children — and most adults under 46 — with no background orthopoxvirus immunity. These factors together produce an infant mortality rate among children under one year that is fourfold higher than among those over 15. Will the EU approval mean children in the DRC can now get the vaccine? Not automatically. EU authorization does not govern procurement or deployment in African countries. What it does enable is a fresh WHO prequalification for the pediatric indication, which allows UN agencies and multilateral procurement bodies to formally purchase doses for children — a step that was not possible when the vaccine was adult-only. But even with WHO prequalification, the bottlenecks that limited the first 651,000 adult doses to 96 percent DRC coverage remain: cold chain infrastructure, last-mile logistics in conflict zones, trained health workers, and funding. Those operational barriers require investment and coordination that goes beyond what a regulatory decision can provide. What is the difference between the mpox vaccine IMVANEX and older smallpox vaccines? IMVANEX (MVA-BN) is a third-generation, non-replicating vaccine. It can enter human cells and trigger an immune response, but a block in the viral lifecycle at the assembly stage prevents it from producing infectious particles that spread within or between people. Older smallpox vaccines like ACAM2000 use live, replicating vaccinia virus, which produces stronger and more durable antibody responses but cannot be safely used in immunocompromised individuals — including children with HIV or severe malnutrition. For the DRC pediatric population, MVA-BN is the only authorized option precisely because it does not carry the replication risk

The County Government of Nakuru has rolled out a massive vaccination exercise against the monkeypox (Mpox) virus. For the next three weeks, the vaccine would be administered in a phased approach in Maa - facebook.com

News Brief
The County Government of Nakuru has initiated a three-week vaccination campaign against the monkeypox (Mpox) virus, targeting high-risk groups in Maai Mahiu, Longonot, and Salgaa. The program, led by County Executive Committee Member Ms Roselyn Mungai, focuses on individuals with close contact with confirmed cases, long-distance truck drivers, sex workers, and healthcare workers. The vaccine is reported to be up to 82% effective in reducing disease severity, transmission, and mortality. The initiative is part of a broader public health response, complementing existing measures to contain the Mpox outbreak. Vaccination sites are strategically located at truck stopovers in the targeted areas, and the Department of Health is conducting laboratory tests, contact tracing, and isolation of new cases. The campaign emphasizes protecting vulnerable populations due to their increased exposure risk. The vaccination effort is described as a milestone in the county's disease surveillance and outbreak preparedness strategy.
Original source text (English)
Log In Log In Forgot Account? Kenya News Agency - KNA's Post Kenya News Agency - KNA 4d · The County Government of Nakuru has rolled out a massive vaccination exercise against the monkeypox (Mpox) virus. For the next three weeks, the vaccine would be administered in a phased approach in Maai Mahiu, Longonot and Salgaa areas, targeting high-risk groups. According to County Executive Committee Member (CECM) for Health services Ms Roselyn Mungai, the exercise will focus on those at highest risk, including people who have had close physical contact with confirmed cases, populations at increased risk such as long-distance truck drivers and sex workers and healthcare workers who provide direct care to Mpox patients. Ms Mungai assured members of the public that the vaccine, which has been shown to be up to 82 percent effective in reducing disease severity, transmission and mortality, was safe. The CECM said the roll-out is an important milestone and will serve as an additional public health measure to complement a comprehensive response to the Mpox outbreak as well as other measures already in place.. The vaccination programme is being implemented through the collaborative efforts of the Sub-County Health Management Teams, Sub-County AIDS and STI Coordinators (SCASCOs) and healthcare workers, who are not only administering vaccines but also educating communities on Mpox prevention and the importance of seeking care early. “Under the leadership of Governor Susan Kihika, Nakuru County continues to strengthen disease surveillance, outbreak preparedness and preventive healthcare, while encouraging all eligible residents in the targeted areas to receive the Mpox vaccine at the designated vaccination sites,” stated the CECM. She added, "The Mpox vaccination campaign will not target the general population but will prioritize high-risk groups. This vaccine is not a silver bullet but one of several public health measures being used as part of a comprehensive response to the outbreak. It complements our broader efforts to contain and manage mpox in Kenya." In addition to vaccination and screening efforts, the County Government’s Department of Health is conducting laboratory tests to identify and isolate new cases and conducting regular contact tracing. “The campaign will prioritize truck drivers, cross-border PSV drivers and sex workers, with vaccination offered at fixed wellness centers strategically located at truck stopovers in Mai Mahiu and Salgaa along the Northern Transport Corridor,” disclosed Ms Mungai. The CECM said the initiative is designed to protect the most vulnerable groups who are at increased risk of exposure due to the nature of their work and mobility. Mpox was first confirmed in Kenya in 2023, with sporadic cases occurring throughout the year. However, the situation has deteriorated in recent months, requiring a more urgent and coordinated response. All reactions: 2 1 share Like Comment All reactions: 2 1 share Like Comment

B routine priorityPublic-health news

Bavarian Nordic Receives European Commission Approval of Mpox/Smallpox Vaccine for Children Aged 2 to Less than 12 Years - Yahoo Finance

News Brief
Bavarian Nordic A/S has received European Commission approval for its Mpox/Smallpox vaccine IMVANEX® (MVA-BN) to be used in children aged 2 to less than 12 years. This decision follows a Phase 2 clinical study (NCT06549530) involving 227 children and 224 adults, which demonstrated non-inferior immune responses and a similar safety profile in children compared to adults after receiving two standard doses. The approval expands the vaccine's marketing authorization and strengthens its role in global public health preparedness. The European Commission's adoption of the Committee for Medicinal Products for Human Use (CHMP) positive opinion indicates regulatory confidence in the vaccine's safety and efficacy for this age group. No specific case counts, death counts, or risk levels were reported in the evidence provided. The approval is a formal regulatory action and does not suggest ongoing investigation or uncertainty regarding the vaccine's performance in children.

Bavarian Nordic Receives European Commission Approval of Mpox/Smallpox Vaccine for Children Aged 2 to Less than 12 Years Bavarian Nordic A/S Fri, August 7, 2026 at 1:00 AM CDT 3 min read BAVA.CO +0.80% Bavarian Nordic A/S MVA-BN approval expanded to include children aged 2 to less than 12 years for protection against mpox, smallpox and disease caused by vaccinia virus Approval further strengthens MVA-BN's position in supporting global public health preparedness COPENHAGEN, Denmark, August 7, 2026 – Bavarian Nordic A/S (OMX: BAVA) announced today that the European Commission has adopted the recent Committee for Medicinal Products for Human Use (CHMP) positive opinion recommending the approval of a type II variation for IMVANEX® (MVA-BN) smallpox and mpox vaccine, extending the current marketing authorization to include children 2 to less than 12 years of age. The approval is based on data from a Phase 2 clinical study (NCT06549530) involving 227 children aged 2 to <12 years and 224 adults, demonstrating non-inferior immune responses and a similar safety profile in children compared to adults following vaccination with two standard doses of MVA-BN. The study was co-funded by the Coalition for Epidemic Preparedness Innovations (CEPI). With this approval, MVA-BN is now authorized in the European Union for use in individuals aged 2 years and older, marking a significant expansion of access to a non-replicating vaccine designed to help protect vulnerable populations against mpox, smallpox and disease caused by vaccinia virus. Paul Chaplin, President and Chief Executive Officer of Bavarian Nordic, said: "The European Commission's approval of MVA-BN for children aged 2 to less than 12 years represents an important advancement in our efforts to broaden access to protection against mpox and smallpox. Children have carried a disproportionate burden during recent mpox outbreaks, particularly in parts of Africa, underscoring the importance of making safe and effective vaccines available to this vulnerable population. We are grateful to the study participants, investigators, CEPI and our partners whose collaboration helped generate the data supporting this approval." The approval adds to the growing body of clinical evidence supporting the use of MVA-BN across age groups and further strengthens Bavarian Nordic's commitment to supporting global public health preparedness and outbreak response. About the mpox and smallpox vaccine MVA-BN or Modified Vaccinia Ankara-Bavarian Nordic is the only non-replicating mpox vaccine approved in the U.S., Switzerland, Singapore, Mexico (marketed as JYNNEOS®), Canada (marketed as IMVAMUNE®), the EU/EEA European Economic Area and United Kingdom (marketed as IMVANEX®). Originally developed as a smallpox vaccine in collaboration with the U.S. government to ensure the supply of a smallpox vaccine for the entire population, including immunocompromised individuals who are not recommended vaccination with traditional replicating smallpox vaccines, MVA-BN has been indicated for use in the general population (from 2 years old) in individuals considered at risk for smallpox or mpox infection. Story Continues About Bavarian Nordic Bavarian Nordic is a global vaccine company with a mission to improve health and save lives through innovative vaccines. We are a preferred supplier of mpox and smallpox vaccines to governments to enhance public health preparedness and have a leading portfolio of travel vaccines. B routine priorityPublic-health news

WHO: Mpox Is Not the New COVID, But We Must Act Now - Dagens.com

News Brief
The World Health Organization (WHO) has stated that mpox, in either its new or old strain, is not the next pandemic like COVID-19, but it is crucial to act now to control its spread. During a UN media briefing on August 20, 2024, WHO representative Hans Kluge emphasized that the organization is well-equipped to manage the outbreak. The WHO declared the outbreak a public health emergency of international concern after identifying the new Clade 1b variant, which has raised global concerns due to its easier transmission through close contact. Sweden confirmed its first case of the Clade 1b variant last week, marking the first instance of its spread outside Africa. The European region is reporting approximately 100 new cases of the Clade 2 Mpox strain monthly. Kluge also noted that focusing on the new Clade 1b variant will strengthen efforts against the less severe Clade 2 variant, which has been spreading globally since 2022. The response includes improved health guidance and monitoring, with the WHO urging the implementation of systems to control and eliminate mpox worldwide.

担心传染性 猴痘 菌株会导致痛苦的水疱疹在英国“快速”蔓延

Fears contagious mpox strain that causes painful blister rash silently spreading in UK amid ‘rapid’ outbreak
新闻简报最近的一份报告强调了英国(尤其是伦敦)猴痘 病例的令人担忧的增加,今年至少记录了 100 例。北爱尔兰卫生官员于 2026 年 6 月证实了“暴发 迅速发展”,并指出这是英国四个国家中每月 Ib 分支 猴痘 病例总数最高的。由于未被发现的感染和延迟的 诊断,暴发 被描述为“悄然传播”,有 5 名患者需要住院 治疗。英国卫生安全局 (UKHSA) 报告 2025 年仅报告 21 例病例,表明数量大幅增加。英国国民医疗服务体系(NHS)敦促符合条件的个人,尤其是与其他男性发生性行为的男性以及最近接触过的男性,接种疫苗。专家警告说,持续的人传人 传播 可能会导致更多的集群和 突变,从而增加病毒的传播能力。暴发 与 2023-2024 年出现的 Ib 分支有关,主要在中部非洲传播,但专家认为,由于医疗保健条件更好,英国等发达国家的死亡率较低。
查看新闻五要素
时间
文章发表于2026-08-04。据指出,北爱尔兰的 暴发 于 2026 年 6 月之前开始,其中 Ib 分支 猴痘 确诊病例的月度最高总数是在 暴发 的第一个月。
地点与对象
暴发报告发生在英国北爱尔兰,病例主要集中在伦敦。受影响人群包括男性,平均年龄 41 岁,大多数病例发生在英国,尤其是伦敦。
事件
专家们对英国 猴痘 病例的增加表示担忧,北爱尔兰的一份报告证实了“猴痘 病例的迅速发展”,并且 6 月份发现了至少 22 例病例。暴发 归因于 Ib 猴痘 分支,该分支正在社区中悄然传播。
规模、影响与风险
截至今年,英国至少有 100 例 1b 猴痘 病例,其中大多数发生在伦敦。2025 年,仅记录了 21 起案件。北爱尔兰的 暴发 因其相对于人口的规模而被描述为特别引人注目。5 名患者需要住院 治疗,由于未发现感染和延迟 诊断,进一步聚集性聚集的风险凸显。
应对、状态与不确定性
北爱尔兰卫生官员已确认 暴发,英国卫生安全局 (UKHSA) 已提供病例数数据。NHS 正在向高危人群提供 猴痘 疫苗,包括与其他男性发生性行为的男性以及最近与感染者接触过的男性。文章指出,暴发 正在“悄然传播”,持续推广预暴露 暴发 对于高危人群非常重要。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Fears contagious mpox strain that causes painful blister rash silently spreading in UK amid ‘rapid’ outbreak

News Brief
A recent report highlights a concerning rise in mpox cases in the UK, particularly in London, with at least 100 cases recorded this year. Health officials in Northern Ireland confirmed a 'rapidly developing outbreak' in June 2026, noting that it was the highest monthly total of clade Ib mpox cases among the UK's four nations. The outbreak is described as 'silently spreading' due to undetected infections and delayed diagnosis, with five patients requiring hospital treatment. The UK Health Security Agency (UKHSA) reported only 21 cases in 2025, indicating a significant increase. The NHS is urging eligible individuals, especially men who have sex with other men and those with recent contact, to get vaccinated. Experts warn that sustained human-to-human transmission could lead to more clusters and mutations, increasing the virus's transmissibility. The outbreak is linked to clade Ib, which emerged in 2023-2024 and has been spreading mainly in central Africa, but experts believe the fatality rates in developed nations like the UK are lower due to better healthcare access.
Original source text (English)
EXPERTS have sounded the alarm over a concerning rise in mpox cases in the UK. Scientists today told Sun Health that the “continued detection” of cases suggest the virus is spreading silently” . A report published last week by health officials in Northern Ireland confirmed it was aware of a “rapidly developing outbreak” in the country Credit: Alamy Latest figures suggest that there have been at least 100 cases of the clade 1b mutation in the UK so far this year, the majority in London. By comparison, just 21 cases were logged across the entirety of 2025, according to the UK Health Security Agency (UKHSA). Sign up for the Health newsletter Thank you! A report published last week by health officials in Northern Ireland also confirmed it was aware of a “rapidly developing outbreak” in the country, accounting for at least 22 cases in June. Most of the cases had no travel abroad in recent weeks, suggesting spread in the community. Anyone eligible for the mpox vaccine in the UK has been urged to get it, to thwart any potential spread of the virus across the country. A different, less severe, form of mpox, called clade 2, sparked alarm in 2022, when it spread globally, mainly through sexual contact among gay and bisexual men. However, new infections gradually tapered off amid boosted awareness of the disease and a hurried vaccination program. At the time the UK reported almost 4,000 cases, with NHS England inoculating over 68,000 people against the disease with the smallpox vaccine, which works due to the two viruses being closely related. According to the new report from the Public Health Agency in Belfast , all cases were men with an average age of 41. Six had reported previously having at least one dose of the vaccine, with 12 confirming they had not had a vaccine. Five of the patients required hospital treatment. Writing in the journal Eurosurveillance , the scientists said: “ Before June 2026, there had been no detection of mpox infection in Northern Ireland for over 12 months, and, within this same period, notification of clinically suspected cases had been relatively infrequent. “ In the first month of this outbreak, Northern Ireland recorded the highest monthly total of clade Ib mpox confirmed cases of the four nations in the UK. “ In the context of recent UK case numbers, the size of this outbreak – 22 confirmed cases – is particularly notable, given the relatively small population size of Northern Ireland .” Mpox and the types explained MPOX is a viral infectious disease. It is a rare zoonosis, which means that it is transmitted to humans from an animal. The disease primarily occurs in remote parts of Central and West Africa , near tropical rainforests. Mpox causes symptoms between five and 21 days after exposure. The symptoms of monkeypox start similar to the flu . But then a rash breaks out, starting on the face. Initial symptoms include: An itchy and sometimes painful rash slowly appears one to three days into illness, starting with the face, palms of the hands and soles of the feet. What starts as a seemingly harmless red rash – which looks like chickenpox – evolves into angry blisters that can last several days. The inside of the mouth, genitals and lining of the eyes can also be affected by the nasty rash. If the rash spreads to the eyes it can cause blindness. While some patients may have only a few lesions, others will have thousands, the WHO says. Symptoms generally last from 14 to 21 days, with severe cases relating to age, the extent of virus exposure, the patient’s health and the severity of complications. What are the different mpox Clades? There are two distinct mpox Clades: Clade 1 (with subclades 1a and 1b) Clade 2 (with subclades 2a and 2b) Clade 2 (subclade 2b) was responsible for the global mpox outbreak in 2022-2023. Over 100,000 cases were reported across the world. Clade 1b emerged in 2023-2024 and has swept mainly through central Africa, infecting tens of thousands since the outbreak began. Countries like the Democratic Republic of Congo have been hit especially hard with cases also spotted in Burundi, Rwanda, Uganda, and Kenya. The World Health Organization (WHO) declared the clade 1b outbreak a Public Health Emergency of International Concern (PHEIC) in August 2024, due to rapid expansion and higher severity compared to clade 2. They added: “Our experience demonstrates the potential for a rapid growth in case numbers over a short time period and emphasises the need for increased public awareness of the ongoing risks of acquiring mpox as an STI in Europe, particularly during the summer months, when travel-associated infections are more likely. “Continued promotion of pre-exposure vaccination remains important for groups at highest risk of mpox.” Dr Jonas D. Albarnaz, an expert in infectious disease at The Pirbright Institute in Woking, told Sun Health : “The continued detection of cases indicates that some cases remain undetected, enabling the virus to spread silently. “Cases are usually identified when individuals report to sexual health clinics or emergency services due to worsening of the symptoms. “Approximately half of the individuals sought emergency services first, which may have delayed diagnosis in a few days. “Undetected infections, delayed diagnosis, and ongoing community transmission make the emergence of more clusters of clade 1b mpox likely.” He added: “One important consideration of the community transmission of mpox is that sustained human-to-human transmission offers an opportunity for the virus to acquire mutations that increase the efficiency of transmission. “Both clade 1b and II mpox viruses have patterns of mutations that indicate these viruses evolved during sustained human-to-human transmission.” Professor Michael Marks, a professor of medicine at the London School of Hygiene & Tropical Medicine, also said: “Close sexual contact is clearly an effective route of transmission for both clade 1 and 2 mpox. “We have seen following the 2022 clade 2 pandemic that there has been low level sustained transmission in the UK and we should anticipate that this may be similar for clade 1.” Clade 1b emerged in 2024 and has swept mainly through central Africa infecting tens of thousands since the outbreak began. Countries like the Democratic Republic of Congo have been hit especially hard with cases also spotted in Burundi, Rwanda, Uganda, and Kenya. However, experts say fatality rates of clade 1b from central Africa are unlikely to be replicated in developed nations like the UK due to better access to higher quality healthcare. Following the emergence of cases, the World Health Organization (WHO) declared an ongoing mpox outbreak across several central African nations as a “public health emergency of international concern”. Can I get the jab? THE NHS is offering the mpox vaccine to people who are at increased risk of catching mpox. You may be able to have the mpox vaccine if: you’re a man who has sex with other men and you have multiple partners, participate in group sex, or attend sex-on-premises venues (staff at these venues may also be eligible) you’ve had contact with someone who has mpox If you’ve had contact with someone who has mpox, the contact tracing team should contact you. They will check your risk of catching mpox and tell you if you need to have an mpox vaccine. This is the same designation the WHO gave Covid in late January 2020, just a few weeks before the virus ripped across the world. Mpox causes characteristic lumpy lesions, as well as a fever, aches and pains and fatigue. However, in a small number of cases, it can enter the blood and lungs, as well as other parts of the body, when it becomes life-threatening. Current mpox vaccines , which are designed to work on smallpox a close relative of the mpox virus, were used during the 2022 outbreak against the milder strain. But experts believe it does offer some protection against 1b. The WHO and the NHS recommends a vaccine within four days of contact with someone who has the virus or within up to 14 days if there are no symptoms. Healthcare workers and men who have sex with men are advised to receive a vaccine even if they have had no mpox exposure. There are no direct treatments available with medics focused instead on supporting a patient to help their body fight off the virus. Dr Albarnaz said: “MVA-BN is one of the vaccines currently available globally and requires the intradermal injection of two doses four weeks apart. “However, immunity wanes post-vaccination, suggesting that boosters might be required to maintain vaccine effectiveness.”
